93	## baselines
94	
95	### baselines.RandomPlayer: Random player
96	`class RandomPlayer(Agent): name = "random"`
97	- **Invariants:** Snake; draws uniformly from the 3 moves with the given `rng`, whatever `explore` is; `scores` is `None` (BASE-01)
98	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomPlayer/ · **Cases:** 30 recorded moves from rng seed 3
99	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
100	
101	### baselines.ScriptedBot: Scripted bot
102	`class ScriptedBot(Agent): name = "scripted"`
103	- **Invariants:** Snake; reads only obs via `snake.Fact`; a safe move toward the food, else the closest safe move, else straight; ties by straight, left, right; `scores` is `None` (BASE-02, BASE-03)
104	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedBot/ · **Cases:** food left with left free; all moves fatal
105	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
106	
107	### baselines.RandomSwimmer: Random swimmer
108	`class RandomSwimmer(Agent): name = "random"`
109	- **Invariants:** the tank; for each row, a direction uniform on the circle and a speed uniform in [0, 2.5] from the given `rng`, whatever `explore` is; `scores` is `None` (BASE-04)
110	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomSwimmer/ · **Cases:** 30 recorded 5-fish moves from rng seed 3
111	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
112	
113	### baselines.FleeBot: Scripted fleeing bot
114	`class FleeBot(Agent): name = "scripted"`
115	- **Invariants:** the tank; reads only obs via `tank.View`; swims at 2.5 directly away from the ball's relative position when `BALL_SEEN`, else `(0, 0)`; `scores` is `None` (BASE-05, BASE-06)
116	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.FleeBot/ · **Cases:** ball seen at (1, 0); ball unseen
117	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
118	
119	## tabular
120	
121	### tabular.TabularSettings: type
122	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TabularSettings: lr: float; gamma: float; explore: Explore`
123	- **Invariants:** `0 < lr ≤ 1`; `0 ≤ gamma < 1`
124	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tabular.TabularSettings/ · **Cases:** lr 0.1, gamma 0.9, explore 1.0 → 0.01 over 50,000
125	- **Used by:** registry
126	
127	### tabular.TabularQ: Table-based Q-learner
128	`class TabularQ(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: TabularSettings, n_obs: int = 11, n_actions: int = 3) -> None`
129	- **Invariants:** discrete moves only; one row of scores per distinct obs, starting at 0; `observe` applies `Q ← Q + lr·(r + gamma·max Q(next) − Q)`, with no next term when `done` (TAB-01); saves `.npz`
130	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the obs length isn't `n_obs`
131	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tabular.TabularQ/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed update; a game-ending update
132	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
133	
134	## dqn
135	
136	### dqn.DQNSettings: type
137	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class DQNSettings: rung: Literal["plain", "replay", "target", "double"]; lr: float; gamma: float; hidden: tuple[int, ...]; explore: Explore; batch_size: int; buffer_size: int; target_every: int`
138	- **Invariants:** each rung adds one piece to the one before (AD-03); `plain` ignores `batch_size`, `buffer_size`, `target_every`; `hidden` defaults to `(64, 64)`
139	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/dqn.DQNSettings/ · **Cases:** one per rung, all other fields equal
140	- **Used by:** registry
141	
142	### dqn.DQNAgent: DQN learner
143	`class DQNAgent(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: DQNSettings, n_obs: int, n_actions: int, torch_seed: int) -> None`
144	- **Invariants:** discrete moves only; CPU only; target `r + gamma·Q′(next, a*)`: `Q′` is the learner for `plain`/`replay`, the frozen copy for `target`/`double`; `a*` is `Q′`'s argmax, except the learner's under `double`; no next term when `done`; batches wait for a full store; the copy refreshes every `target_every` updates (DQN-01–DQN-04); saves `.pt`
145	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `buffer_size < batch_size` from `replay` up
146	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/dqn.DQNAgent/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed target per rung
147	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
148	
149	## pg
150	
151	### pg.PGSettings: type
152	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PGSettings: rung: Literal["reinforce", "baseline", "actor_critic", "ppo"]; lr: float; critic_lr: float; gamma: float; hidden: tuple[int, ...]; init_log_spread: float; steps_per_update: int; passes: int; minibatch: int; clip: float`
153	- **Invariants:** each rung adds one piece to the one before (AD-12); `reinforce` ignores `critic_lr`, `steps_per_update`, `passes`, `minibatch`, `clip`; `baseline` adds `critic_lr`; `actor_critic` adds `steps_per_update`; `ppo` adds `passes`, `minibatch`, `clip`; `hidden` defaults to `(64, 64)`; `0 ≤ gamma < 1`, `0 < clip < 1`
154	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGSettings/ · **Cases:** one per rung, all other fields equal
155	- **Used by:** registry
156	
157	### pg.PGAgent: Policy-gradient learner
158	`class PGAgent(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: PGSettings, n_obs: int, action_size: int, torch_seed: int) -> None`
159	- **Invariants:** continuous moves only; CPU only; a diagonal Gaussian policy per obs row, log spread clamped to [−5, 1]; `act(explore=True)` samples with `rng`, `explore=False` returns the mean (PG-07); `scores` is the mean of the two spreads; learns from the sampled move; `reinforce`/`baseline` update once every agent's experience has ended, `actor_critic`/`ppo` every `steps_per_update` steps; weights per PG-01–PG-04, no next term when `done`; every fish's transitions train the one policy (PG-06); every update goes through the `pg` functions below, and `last_update: UpdateLog | None` holds the latest one, `None` before the first (AD-15); saves `.pt`
160	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `minibatch > steps_per_update` under `ppo`
161	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGAgent/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed update per rung; a PPO move already past the clip
162	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01
163	
164	### pg.UpdateLog: type
165	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class UpdateLog: rung: str; weights: torch.Tensor; log_probs: torch.Tensor; values: torch.Tensor | None; critic_loss: tuple[float, float] | None; minibatches: tuple[torch.Tensor, ...]`
166	- **Invariants:** one entry per move the update learned from, in fish order then time order; `weights` from `move_weights`; `log_probs` from `gaussian_log_prob` at the sampled move, before training changes the policy; `values` and `critic_loss` (squared error to the targets before and after the critic's step) are `None` under `reinforce`; `minibatches` is `minibatch_plan`'s output under `ppo`, and one index tensor covering every move otherwise (AD-15)
167	- **Boundary:** tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.UpdateLog/ · **Cases:** a REINFORCE update over a 12-step 1-fish game; a PPO update over a 5-fish batch
168	- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29
169	
170	### pg.discounted_returns: Discounted returns
171	`def discounted_returns(rewards: torch.Tensor, gamma: float) -> torch.Tensor`
172	- **Invariants:** `rewards` float32 `(T,)` is one fish's moves in time order, its experience ending at the last move; returns `G_t = Σ_k gamma^k r_{t+k}`, same shape and dtype (PG-01)
173	- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29
174	
175	### pg.move_weights: Each rung's weight per move
176	`def move_weights(rung: str, rewards: torch.Tensor, done: torch.Tensor, values: torch.Tensor | None, next_values: torch.Tensor | None, gamma: float) -> torch.Tensor`
177	- **Invariants:** inputs `(T,)`, one fish's moves in time order; `reinforce`: `G_t`; `baseline`: `G_t − V(s_t)`; `actor_critic`, `ppo`: `r_t + gamma·V(s_{t+1}) − V(s_t)`, no next term where `done` (PG-01–PG-04); never part of the gradient
178	- **Errors:** `ValueError` for an unknown rung, or `values`/`next_values` missing where the rung needs them
179	- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29
180	
181	### pg.gaussian_log_prob: Log-probability of a swim
182	`def gaussian_log_prob(mean: torch.Tensor, log_spread: torch.Tensor, action: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor`
183	- **Invariants:** inputs `(N, size)`, `log_spread` already clamped to [−5, 1]; returns `(N,)` `Σ_i [−½((a_i − μ_i)/σ_i)² − log σ_i − ½ log 2π]`, differentiable in `mean` and `log_spread`
184	- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29
185	
186	### pg.ppo_objective: Clipped objective per move
187	`def ppo_objective(log_prob: torch.Tensor, old_log_prob: torch.Tensor, weight: torch.Tensor, clip: float) -> torch.Tensor`
188	- **Invariants:** inputs `(N,)`; `ρ = exp(log_prob − old_log_prob)`; returns `(N,)` `min(ρ·A, clip(ρ, 1 − clip, 1 + clip)·A)`; a move with `ρ > 1 + clip` and `A > 0`, or `ρ < 1 − clip` and `A < 0`, has gradient exactly 0 (PG-04)
189	- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29
190	
191	### pg.minibatch_plan: Passes over a batch
192	`def minibatch_plan(n_moves: int, passes: int, minibatch: int, gen: torch.Generator) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, ...]`
193	- **Invariants:** `passes` passes in order, each a fresh permutation of `range(n_moves)` from `gen` cut into minibatches of `minibatch` (the last may be shorter); every move appears once per pass (PG-04)
194	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if any argument is below 1
195	- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29
196	
197	## registry
198	
199	### registry.UnknownName: type
200	`class UnknownName(ValueError): kind: str; name: str; choices: tuple[str, ...]`
201	- **Invariants:** `kind` is `env`, `setup`, `learner` or `run`; `choices` lists the valid names for that environment; the message lists them (RUN-01 edge case)
202	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.UnknownName/ · **Cases:** an unknown reward; a Snake learner asked for in the tank
203	- **Used by:** cli, train
204	
205	### registry.make_env: Build an environment
206	`def make_env(env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str]) -> Env`
207	- **Invariants:** a fresh environment per call; `env` in `ENVS` (`snake`, `tank`; tests register a second); `setup` is `{"reward": "plain" | "shaped" | "none"}` for Snake (`none` plays plain, for baselines) and `{"fish": 1 | 5, "view": "short" | "wide"}` for the tank
208	- **Errors:** `UnknownName` for an unknown environment, setup key or setup value
209	- **Boundary:** train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_env/ · **Cases:** `snake`/`shaped`; `tank`/5 fish/`wide`; an unknown reward; `reward` given to the tank
210	- **Used by:** train, replay; PL-01, PL-03
211	
212	### registry.default_params: A learner's full settings
213	`def default_params(learner: str) -> dict[str, int | float | str]`
214	- **Invariants:** every setting of the learner, flattened as `RunSettings.params` (`lr`, `explore.start`…); `{}` for `random` and `scripted`; the four `dqn-*` names differ only in `rung`, and so do the four `pg-*` names (AD-03, AD-10, AD-12)
215	- **Errors:** `UnknownName`
216	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.default_params/ · **Cases:** `tabular`; `dqn-double`; `pg-ppo`; `scripted`
217	- **Used by:** cli
218	
219	### registry.make_agent: Build an agent from settings
220	`def make_agent(learner: str, params: Mapping[str, int | float | str], *, env: str, n_obs: int, actions: ActionSpace, seed: int) -> Agent`
221	- **Invariants:** Snake's learners are `tabular`, `dqn-plain`, `dqn-replay`, `dqn-target`, `dqn-double`; the tank's are `pg-reinforce`, `pg-baseline`, `pg-actor-critic`, `pg-ppo`; `random` and `scripted` build that environment's baselines; `params` holds every `default_params(learner)` key (AD-10); learners return a `Learner`
222	- **Errors:** `UnknownName`, its choices the environment's own names, when `learner` is unknown or belongs to the other environment; `ValueError` for invalid or missing params
223	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_agent/ · **Cases:** `tabular` with defaults; `pg-ppo` with defaults; `dqn-triple`; `dqn-plain` for the tank
224	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
225	
226	### registry.load_agent: Load a saved agent
227	`def load_agent(record: RunRecord) -> Agent`
228	- **Invariants:** a baseline record rebuilds its environment's baseline; a learner record loads its agent file
229	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the missing file
230	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.load_agent/ · **Cases:** a saved table learner; a scripted-bot record; a saved PPO policy
231	- **Used by:** replay; PL-03
232	
233	## evaluate
234	
235	### evaluate.TestGame: type
236	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TestGame: seed: int; moves: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; scores: tuple[np.ndarray | None, ...]; score_after: tuple[float, ...]; ending: str; score: float`
237	- **Invariants:** one `moves`, `scores` and `score_after` entry per step; each `moves` entry is that step's `Env.step` input, rows of agents not acting 0; `scores` entries `(n_agents, len(score_names))`, rows of agents not acting NaN, or `None` for baselines; `score == score_after[-1]`
238	- **Boundary:** train, store, replay, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.TestGame/ · **Cases:** a table-learner game ending on a wall; a scripted-bot game ending starved; a one-move game; a 5-fish PPO game reaching the cap
239	- **Used by:** train, store, replay, compare
240	
241	### evaluate.TestResult: type
242	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TestResult: games: tuple[TestGame, ...]; score: float`
243	- **Invariants:** 100 games, seeds 0–99 in order; `score` is the mean game `score` (EVAL-02)
244	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.TestResult/ · **Cases:** 100 recorded scripted-bot games; 100 recorded flee-bot games
245	- **Used by:** train
246	
247	### evaluate.play_game: Play one greedy game
248	`def play_game(env: Env, agent: Agent, seed: int, rng: np.random.Generator) -> TestGame`
249	- **Invariants:** `act(explore=False)` on every step's obs; `rng` reaches only agents that ignore `explore`; same inputs → same game
250	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.play_game/ · **Cases:** the scripted bot on seed 7; the flee bot with 5 fish on seed 7
251	- **Used by:** replay, train; PL-01, PL-03
252	
253	### evaluate.test_agent: Score an agent on the test games
254	`def test_agent(env: Env, agent: Agent, *, rng_seed: int = 0) -> TestResult`
255	- **Invariants:** plays test seeds 0–99 of the given environment setup, never used in training (EVAL-01); scores by `Step.score`, never the training reward (SHAPE-04)
256	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.test_agent/ · **Cases:** the scripted bot's 100 recorded games; the flee bot's 100 recorded 1-fish games
257	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
258	
259	## store
260	
261	### store.RunSettings: type
262	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunSettings: env: str; learner: str; setup: Mapping[str, int | str]; seed: int; train_steps: int; params: Mapping[str, int | float | str]; curve_points: int = 20; max_minutes: float = 60.0`
263	- **Invariants:** `setup` as `registry.make_env` takes it, so Snake's names its reward (SHAPE-03) and the tank's its fish count and view; `params` holds every setting of the learner, flattened (`explore.start`, `lr`…): `default_params(learner)` with the user's overrides (AD-10); `train_steps` counts environment steps; saved with library versions, enough to recreate the run (RUN-03); a baseline has `train_steps=0`, Snake's with reward `none`
264	- **Boundary:** cli, train, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.RunSettings/ · **Cases:** a tabular plain run; a double-DQN shaped run; a scripted-bot record; a 5-fish wide-view PPO run; a 1-fish flee-bot record
265	- **Used by:** cli, train, compare
266	
267	### store.CurvePoint: type
268	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePoint: steps: int; score: float; elapsed_s: float`
269	- **Invariants:** `steps` strictly increasing within a run; `score` is a `TestResult.score`
270	- **Boundary:** train, compare, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.CurvePoint/ · **Cases:** the 20 points of a recorded tabular run
271	- **Used by:** train, compare, cli
272	
273	### store.RunRecord: type
274	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: run_id: str; path: Path; settings: RunSettings; status: Literal["complete", "incomplete", "over_time"]; curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]; missing: tuple[str, ...]`
275	- **Invariants:** `complete` means all five pieces of evidence are present (RUN-02); `missing` names absent files; a folder left `running` reads as `incomplete`
276	- **Boundary:** train, compare, replay, registry, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a complete run; an over-time run; a run missing its test games after a disk error; a complete tank run
277	- **Used by:** train, compare, replay, registry, cli
278	
279	### store.StagedRun: type
280	`class StagedRun(Protocol): run_id: str; path: Path; def add_point(self, point: CurvePoint) -> None; def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete", "over_time"], *, agent: Agent | None, games: Sequence[TestGame]) -> RunRecord`
281	- **Invariants:** `add_point` writes at once; `finish` writes the agent and games (moves as exact JSON numbers, AD-06), then `status.json` last; every file written by rename; a failed write gives `incomplete` with `missing` set
282	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.StagedRun/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a 3-point run finished complete; one finished incomplete after an `OSError`
283	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
284	
285	### store.begin_run: Start a run folder
286	`def begin_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> StagedRun`
287	- **Invariants:** a new folder `<env>/<learner>/<setup>/seed-<n>/<UTC timestamp>-<id>/` every time, never reused (RUN-05); `<setup>` is the reward in Snake and `fish-<n>-<view>` in the tank; writes `settings.json` and a `running` status
288	- **Errors:** `OSError` if the folder can't be created
289	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.begin_run/ · **Cases:** two runs with identical settings; a tank run's folder name
290	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
291	
292	### store.list_runs: Find runs
293	`def list_runs(*, root: Path = Path("runs"), env: str | None = None, learner: str | None = None, setup: str | None = None, complete_only: bool = False) -> list[RunRecord]`
294	- **Invariants:** `setup` is the folder label; sorted by environment, learner, setup, seed, run id; an unreadable folder comes back `incomplete` with `missing` set, never skipped
295	- **Boundary:** compare, replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.list_runs/ · **Cases:** a runs folder with one complete, one over-time and one broken run; one with Snake and tank runs
296	- **Used by:** compare, replay, cli; PL-02, PL-03
297	
298	### store.load_test_games: Read a run's test games
299	`def load_test_games(record: RunRecord) -> list[TestGame]`
300	- **Invariants:** seeds 0–99 in order, exactly as saved, moves bit for bit (AD-06)
301	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run and file
302	- **Boundary:** replay, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.load_test_games/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake run's 100 games; a complete 5-fish run's 100 games
303	- **Used by:** replay, compare; PL-02, PL-03
304	
305	## train
306	
307	### train.train_run: One training run
308	`def train_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs"), progress: Callable[[CurvePoint], None] | None = None) -> RunRecord`
309	- **Invariants:** one learner, one environment setup, one seed (RUN-01); each step, one `act` on every acting agent's row and one `observe` per acting agent, in agent order (PG-06, AD-11); `curve_points` evenly spaced test rounds, each passed to `progress` (RUN-04); a baseline plays the test games once; stops at `max_minutes` as `over_time` (RUN-07); Ctrl-C ends `incomplete` (RUN-06); same settings → same curve (RUN-03)
310	- **Errors:** `UnknownName` before any folder exists; a save failure returns an `incomplete` record
311	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train_run/ · **Cases:** a recorded 3-point tabular run; an over-time run; a recorded 3-point 5-fish PPO run
312	- **Used by:** cli; PL-01
313	
314	## compare
315	
316	### compare.Comparison: type
317	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: comparison_id: str; env: str; kind: Literal["baseline", "ladder"]; a: str; b: str; outcome: Literal["beats", "ties", "loses", "better", "worse", "no_difference"]; test: str; p_adjusted: float; explanation: str | None`
318	- **Invariants:** `a` and `b` name a learner and setup, or a baseline and setup; explained exactly when `explanation` is non-empty (EVAL-06); `comparison_id` names the explanation file under `reports/<env>/explanations/`
319	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Comparison/ · **Cases:** an explained tie; an unexplained no-difference; a beat; a tank ladder step
320	- **Used by:** cli
321	
322	### compare.Report: type
323	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Report: env: str; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; waiting: tuple[str, ...]; done: bool; not_done: tuple[str, ...]; path: Path`
324	- **Invariants:** one per environment (AD-14); `waiting` names each comparison not made and the seeds or settings blocking it (EVAL-04); `done` exactly when EVAL-07 holds for `env`, else `not_done` names each gap
325	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Report/ · **Cases:** Snake done with an explained tie; waiting on two seeds; the tank not done because short-view PPO loses to the flee bot
326	- **Used by:** cli
327	
328	### compare.Chart: type
329	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Chart: env: str; learner: str; setup: str; path: Path`
330	- **Invariants:** one per learner and setup with complete runs, the baseline lines from the same setup (BASE-07); `path` is a PNG under `reports/<env>/`, linked from that report (EVAL-03, AD-09)
331	- **Boundary:** compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Chart/ · **Cases:** a tabular plain chart; a double-DQN shaped chart; a 5-fish wide-view PPO chart
332	- **Used by:** compare; PL-02
333	
334	### compare.build_report: Make every comparison
335	`def build_report(*, runs: Path = Path("runs"), out: Path = Path("reports")) -> tuple[Report, ...]`
336	- **Invariants:** one report per environment with runs (AD-14), comparisons through the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill on complete runs only, corrected within that report; EVAL-04 for each learner setup against the baselines of the same setup (BASE-07); EVAL-05 steps — Snake: `tabular`→`dqn-plain`→`dqn-replay`→`dqn-target`→`dqn-double` on `plain`, `dqn-double` `plain`→`shaped`; the tank: `pg-reinforce`→`pg-baseline`→`pg-actor-critic`→`pg-ppo` on `fish-1-short`, `pg-ppo` `fish-1-short`→`fish-5-short`→`fish-5-wide`; top learners `dqn-double` `plain` and `pg-ppo` `fish-5-short` (EVAL-07); a pair whose settings differ beyond the added piece is refused (DQN-05, SHAPE-05, PG-05); writes charts per the `dataviz` skill (EVAL-03)
337	- **Errors:** a refused or short pair goes to `waiting`, never raised
338	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.build_report/ · **Cases:** a runs folder with 5 seeds of two Snake rungs and both baselines; one with 5 seeds of two tank rungs and both tank baselines
339	- **Used by:** cli; PL-02
340	
341	## replay
342	
343	### replay.ReplayGame: type
344	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ReplayGame: title: str; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]; moves: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; scores: tuple[np.ndarray | None, ...]; score_after: tuple[float, ...]; ending: str; actions: ActionSpace`
345	- **Invariants:** one more frame than moves (the start frame); `moves`, `scores` and `score_after` as in `TestGame`
346	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.ReplayGame/ · **Cases:** a 12-move table-learner game; a baseline game; a one-move game; a 40-step 5-fish PPO game with a removal
347	- **Used by:** replay; PL-03
348	
349	### replay.ShowLog: type
350	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ShowLog: frames_shown: int; pauses: int; steps: int; final_speed: int; closed_early: bool`
351	- **Invariants:** `frames_shown` ≤ the game's frames; `final_speed` in 1–30; `closed_early` when the window closed before the last frame
352	- **Boundary:** replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.ShowLog/ · **Cases:** the recorded event script (pause, step, speed up) on a 12-move game; a window closed at the start frame
353	- **Used by:** replay, cli; PL-03
354	
355	### replay.run_replay: Find a run and watch one of its games
356	`def run_replay(run_id: str, *, index: int | None = None, seed: int | None = None, speed: int = 10, runs: Path = Path("runs")) -> ShowLog`
357	- **Invariants:** finds `run_id` among `store.list_runs`; passes `index` and `seed` through to `open_game` unchanged, then `show` (REPLAY-01, REPLAY-07)
358	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming a missing run; `ValueError` if both `index` and `seed` are given; as `open_game` and `show`
359	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.run_replay/ · **Cases:** a fake watching game 0 of a table run; an unknown run id
360	- **Used by:** cli; PL-03
361	
362	### replay.open_game: Rebuild a game to watch
363	`def open_game(record: RunRecord, *, index: int | None = None, seed: int | None = None) -> ReplayGame`
364	- **Invariants:** at most one of `index` (a saved test game, rebuilt from its seed and moves, REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06) or `seed` (the agent plays greedily on a fresh seed of the run's setup, REPLAY-07); neither means saved game 0
365	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if both `index` and `seed` are given; `FileNotFoundError` or `IndexError` naming the missing item; `class ReplayMismatch(RuntimeError): move: int; expected: str; got: str`, at the first step whose game score or ending differs
366	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_game/ · **Cases:** saved game 0 of a table run; neither `index` nor `seed` (saved game 0); seed 12345; a tampered saved game; saved game 3 of a 5-fish run
367	- **Used by:** replay; PL-03
368	
369	### replay.show: Watch a replay
370	`def show(game: ReplayGame, *, speed: int = 10) -> ShowLog`
371	- **Invariants:** a pygame-ce window drawing each frame's shapes and labels, the step number and the ending (REPLAY-02, REPLAY-08); discrete moves: one bar per `score_names` entry with the chosen move marked; continuous moves: an arrow from each acting agent's shape along its move, with its spread (REPLAY-03, REPLAY-09); "n/a" when `scores` is `None`; space pauses and resumes, → steps while paused, ↑/↓ set 1–30 steps per second (REPLAY-04, REPLAY-05); returns what was shown when the window closes
372	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `speed` is outside 1–30
373	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.show/ · **Cases:** a recorded event script (pause, step, speed up) on a 12-move game; the same script on a 5-fish game
374	- **Used by:** replay; PL-03
375	
376	## cli
377	
378	### cli.main: The rllab command
379	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
380	- **Invariants:** `rllab train [--env snake|tank] --learner L [--reward R | --fish F --view V] --seed N [--steps S]` (`--env` defaults to `snake`; a baseline name records it), `rllab compare` (every environment's report), `rllab replay RUN_ID [--game I | --seed N] [--speed V]`; `train` merges the user's overrides into `registry.default_params` before `train.train_run` (AD-10); `replay` calls `replay.run_replay`; one progress line per curve point; 0 on success
381	- **Errors:** 2 with the valid choices on `UnknownName`; 1 naming the missing item on a missing run or file
382	- **Used by:** the `rllab` console script
383

# RL Lab — Roadmap

**Last updated:** 2026-10-18

| Stage | Name | Source | Status* |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | Snake + shared core | brief | Planned |
| S2 | Fish tank | ideas/fish-tank.md | Split |
| S3 | Predator and prey | ideas/predator-prey.md | Specced |

| Milestone | Stage | Name | Packages | Pipelines | Depends | Status* |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M1 | S1 | Train a run | rllab | PL-01 | — | Planned |
| M2 | S1 | Compare and replay | rllab | PL-01, PL-02, PL-03 | M1 (store.RunRecord, PL-01 run folders) | Designed |
| M3 | S2 | Fish tank | rllab | PL-01, PL-02, PL-03 | M1 (train.train_run, registry.make_env), M2 (compare.build_report, replay.run_replay, TC-37's S1-exit Snake snapshot) | Designed |

*Written by `dt roadmap` from doc statuses and todo checkboxes; never hand-edited.

## S1: Snake + shared core
**Outline:** Train table-based Q-learning, then DQN built one piece at a time, on Snake in under an hour on the laptop, compare it with random and scripted baselines, watch a replay, and explain the result.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing, dataviz

## M1: Train a run
**Outline:** `rllab train` trains the table learner or any of the four DQN rungs, or records a baseline, on plain or shaped Snake from one seed, and saves a complete run folder.
**Skills:** —
**References:** —
**Exit:** Every learner and baseline trains to a complete run folder on either reward; `rllab train --learner tabular --reward plain --seed 1 --smoke` passes; TC-01–TC-11 pass.
**Plan:** [../tasks/plan.md](../tasks/plan.md)

## M2: Compare and replay
**Outline:** `rllab compare` reports every baseline and ladder comparison with learning-curve charts and the done check, `rllab replay` shows any saved or fresh game with the agent's move scores, and the DQN defaults are tuned to the 60-minute limit.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing, dataviz
**References:** —
**Exit:** TC-12–TC-20 pass, and TC-21 holds by hand: Double DQN beats random and beats or ties the scripted bot, each run under 60 minutes.

## S2: Fish tank
**Outline:** Train policy-gradient fish, from REINFORCE up to 5-fish PPO, in under an hour per run in the circular tank, compare them with a random swimmer and a scripted fleeing bot, and watch any school of fish as a replay.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing, dataviz

## M3: Fish tank
**Outline:** `rllab train --env tank` trains any policy-gradient rung, REINFORCE to PPO, or records the random swimmer or flee bot, at 1 or 5 fish with the short or wide view; `rllab compare` adds the tank's own report with its ladder and done check; `rllab replay` shows any school of fish with each fish's swim arrow and spread; the PPO defaults are tuned to the 60-minute limit, and saved Snake runs still rerun, compare and replay unchanged.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing, dataviz
**References:** —
**Exit:** TC-22–TC-31 and TC-33–TC-37 pass; `rllab train --env tank --learner pg-reinforce --fish 1 --view short --seed 1 --smoke` passes; TC-32 holds by hand: 5-fish short-view PPO beats the random swimmer and beats or ties the flee bot, each run under 60 minutes.

## S3: Predator and prey
**Outline:** Train a predator and five foraging prey that learn together in the tank, up a ladder from both learning at once to taking turns to training against past versions, with a scripted-opponent control for each side; compare each side with random and scripted bots, chart predator versions against prey versions, and watch any game as a replay.
**Skills:** null-hypothesis-testing, dataviz

# rllab — Pipelines

**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-18 · **Contract:** [contract.md](contract.md)

## PL-01: Train a run
**Entry:** `src/rllab/train/pipeline.py::train_run` · **Trigger:** `rllab train [--env snake|tank] --learner L [--reward R | --fish F --view V] --seed N [--steps S]`
**Purpose:** Train one learner (or record a baseline) on Snake or the fish tank from one seed and save a run folder holding its learning curve, final agent and test games.
**Covers:** SNAKE-01–SNAKE-08, TANK-01–TANK-13, BASE-01–BASE-07, TAB-01–TAB-04, DQN-01–DQN-04, DQN-06, DQN-07, PG-01–PG-04, PG-06–PG-08, SHAPE-01–SHAPE-04, RUN-01–RUN-09, EVAL-01, EVAL-02, DATA-01, QR-01, QR-02

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env, setup → Env | name → constructor dict (`snake`, `tank`), each environment's setup keys and values checked, `UnknownName` listing the choices | RUN-01, RUN-08 |
| 2 | `SnakeEnv` | snake.env | reward → Env | one agent, 10×10 board, 3 relative moves, food from the game's own generator, wall/body/starved endings, the 11 facts, plain reward, food count as the float score, cells in the frame; shaped reward is its upgrade | SNAKE-01–SNAKE-08, SHAPE-01, SHAPE-02, DATA-01, RUN-09 |
| 3 | `TankEnv` | tank.env | fish, view → Env | 1 or 5 fish in a radius-10 circle, AD-13's step order (speed cap, wall stop, ball moved with the exact-point mirror bounce, removal within 0.75), seed-set ball direction and fish spots, the 11 view numbers within the short or wide radius, +0.1 survival reward, TANK-10's mean seconds as the score, `all_removed` and `time_cap` endings, ring and circles in the frame | TANK-01–TANK-13, DATA-01 |
| 4 | `make_agent` | registry.agents | learner, params, env, n_obs, ActionSpace, seed → Agent | name → constructor dict per environment (Snake's five learners, the tank's four, each environment's `random` and `scripted`), missing params rejected, `UnknownName` with the environment's own choices | RUN-01 |
| 5 | `RandomPlayer` | baselines.random | rng → Agent | uniform draw from the 3 moves, `scores` None | BASE-01 |
| 6 | `ScriptedBot` | baselines.scripted | obs → Agent | safe move toward food, else closest safe, else straight, read through `Fact` | BASE-02, BASE-03 |
| 7 | `RandomSwimmer` | baselines.swimmer | rng → Agent | per row, a direction uniform on the circle and a speed uniform in [0, 2.5], `scores` None | BASE-04 |
| 8 | `FleeBot` | baselines.flee | obs → Agent | per row, 2.5 straight away from the ball's relative position when `BALL_SEEN`, else (0, 0), read through `View` | BASE-05, BASE-06 |
| 9 | `TabularQ` | tabular.q | TabularSettings → Learner | dict of 3-score rows keyed by obs, one-step update, linear `Explore`, `.npz` save and load | TAB-01–TAB-04 |
| 10 | `DQNAgent` | dqn.agent | DQNSettings → Learner | plain rung: 11→64→64→3 MLP, batch of one, hand-written target and squared loss, Adam, `.pt` save and load; replay, target and double rungs are its upgrades | DQN-01–DQN-04, DQN-06, DQN-07, QR-02 |
| 11 | `PGAgent` | pg.agent | PGSettings → Learner | REINFORCE rung: 11→64→64→4 MLP as a diagonal Gaussian per row, log spread clamped to [−5, 1], a sample with `rng` in training and the mean in tests, each fish's discounted returns, one update once every agent's experience has ended, hand-written loss computed only through `discounted_returns`, `move_weights` and `gaussian_log_prob`, `last_update` holding each update's `UpdateLog` (AD-15), Adam, `.pt` save and load; baseline, actor-critic and PPO rungs (adding `ppo_objective` and `minibatch_plan`) are its upgrades | PG-01–PG-04, PG-06–PG-08, QR-02 |
| 12 | `begin_run` | store.runs | RunSettings → StagedRun | new `<timestamp>-<id>` folder under the environment and setup label (reward, or `fish-<n>-<view>`), `settings.json` with library versions, `running` status | RUN-02, RUN-03, RUN-05, SHAPE-03, BASE-07 |
| 13 | `RunFolder` | store.staged | RunSettings, path → StagedRun | `add_point` appends to `curve.json`; `finish` writes agent and `test_games.jsonl` (moves as exact JSON numbers), then `status.json` last, each by temp-file rename | RUN-02, RUN-06 |
| 14 | `train_loop` | train.loop | Env, Agent, RunSettings, StagedRun, progress → RunRecord | seeded games (AD-05), each step one `act(explore=True)` on every acting row and one `observe` per acting agent in agent order (AD-11), a test round at each of the evenly spaced curve points, finish complete; a baseline tests once; 60-minute stop and Ctrl-C are its upgrades | RUN-01, RUN-03, RUN-04, RUN-06–RUN-09, PG-06, QR-01 |
| 15 | `test_agent` | evaluate.test | Env, Agent → TestResult | test seeds 0–99 through `play_game`, mean game `score` | EVAL-01, EVAL-02, SHAPE-04 |
| 16 | `play_game` | evaluate.play | Env, Agent, seed, rng → TestGame | greedy moves on every acting row until done, recording each step's moves, scores and game score | TAB-03, DQN-06, PG-07 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def train_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs"),
              progress: Callable[[CurvePoint], None] | None = None) -> RunRecord:
    env = make_env(settings.env, settings.setup)
    agent = make_agent(settings.learner, settings.params, env=settings.env,
                       n_obs=env.obs_size, actions=env.actions, seed=settings.seed)
    staged = begin_run(settings, root=root)
    return train_loop(env, agent, settings, staged, progress)
```
