# rllab — Contract

**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Draft · **Last updated:** 2026-10-18 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)

## core

### core.ActionSpace: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ActionSpace: kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]; size: int; names: tuple[str, ...]; max_norm: float | None; score_names: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - `discrete`: `size` moves named by `names`; a move is an `int` in `[0, size)`; `max_norm` is `None`.
  - `continuous`: a move is a float32 vector of length `size`, its components named by `names`; the environment caps its length at `max_norm`.
  - `score_names` names the numbers `Agent.scores` gives per agent: `straight, left, right` in Snake, `spread` for any fish (AD-11).
- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, registry, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 moves; the tank's 2-vector capped at 2.5; the predator's 2-vector capped at 3.0
- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, registry, evaluate, replay

### core.Side: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Side: name: str; n_agents: int; obs_size: int; actions: ActionSpace`
- **Invariants:**
  - An environment's sides keep a fixed order, and their names are unique within it (AD-16).
  - Snake has one side: `snake`, 1 agent, 11 obs, discrete.
  - The tank has one side: `fish`, 1 or 5 agents, 11 obs, capped at 2.5.
  - Predator-prey has two sides, in this order: `predator` (1 agent, 11 obs, capped at 3.0), then `prey` (5 agents, 14 obs, capped at 2.5).
- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, registry, evaluate, train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Side/ · **Cases:** Snake's side; a 5-fish tank side; predator-prey's two sides
- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, registry, evaluate, train, replay

### core.Step: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; rewards: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; acting: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; ended: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; done: bool; score: tuple[float, ...]; ending: str | None`
- **Invariants:**
  - Every tuple has one entry per side, in `Env.sides` order (AD-16).
  - `obs[s]` is float32 `(n_agents, obs_size)` of side `s`; rows of agents not acting are all 0.
  - `rewards[s]` is float `(n_agents,)`, 0 for agents that didn't act.
  - `acting[s]` is bool: who moves next.
  - `ended[s]` is bool: whose experience ended on this step (removed, caught, killed, or the game ended).
  - `done` holds exactly when no agent of any side acts.
  - `score[s]` is side `s`'s game score so far: Snake's food eaten; the tank's TANK-10 mean seconds survived; predator-prey's `(catches, pellets per prey)` (HUNT-11).
  - `ending` is `None` until `done`, then a name the environment defines: Snake's `wall`, `body`, `starved` or `full_board`; the tank's `all_removed` or `time_cap`; predator-prey's `all_caught` or `time_cap`.
- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, evaluate, train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** an ordinary Snake move; a food move; a wall death; a tank step removing 1 of 5 fish; a tank step reaching the cap; a predator-prey step with a catch and a pellet eaten
- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, evaluate, train, replay

### core.Transition: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: agent: int; obs: np.ndarray; action: int | np.ndarray; reward: float; next_obs: np.ndarray; done: bool`
- **Invariants:**
  - One agent's step; `agent` counts within its side.
  - `obs` and `next_obs` are float32 `(obs_size,)` of its side.
  - `action` is an `int` for discrete moves, or the float32 vector the agent chose (before any cap) for continuous ones.
  - `done` is that agent's `Step.ended`; when `done`, every learner ignores `next_obs`.
- **Boundary:** train, tabular, dqn, pg · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a mid-game Snake move; a game-ending Snake move; a tank fish's move; a tank fish's removal; a predator's catch; a prey's capture
- **Used by:** train, tabular, dqn, pg

### core.Shape: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Shape: kind: Literal["cell", "circle", "ring"]; x: float; y: float; r: float; style: str; agent: tuple[int, int] | None`
- **Invariants:**
  - Positions are in world units. A `cell` is the unit square with corner `(x, y)`, and `r` is unused. A `circle` is filled and a `ring` outlined, centred at `(x, y)` with radius `r`.
  - `style` names a colour: `head`, `body`, `food`, `tank`, `ball`, `fish`, `predator`, `prey` or `pellet`. An unknown style gets a fallback colour.
  - `agent` is set, as a `(side, agent)` pair, on the shape an agent's arrow starts from: Snake's head, each fish (AD-16).
- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Shape/ · **Cases:** a Snake head cell; a fish circle with its agent; the tank ring; a prey circle with its agent
- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, replay

### core.Frame: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: bounds: tuple[float, float, float, float]; y_down: bool; shapes: tuple[Shape, ...]; labels: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - `bounds` is `(x_min, y_min, x_max, y_max)`, and every shape lies inside it.
  - `y_down` holds when y grows downward. Snake: `(0, 0, 10, 10)`, true. The tank and predator-prey: `(-10, -10, 10, 10)`, false.
  - Shapes draw in order.
  - `labels` are name/value lines (REPLAY-02, REPLAY-08, REPLAY-10). Snake: food eaten. The tank: time in seconds, fish swimming, and each removed fish's removal time. Predator-prey: time in seconds, pellets eaten, prey caught, and each caught prey's capture time.
- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** a Snake start frame; a Snake frame at length 5; a 5-fish tank frame after one removal; a predator-prey frame after one catch; a test-env frame
- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, replay

### core.Env: type
`class Env(Protocol): name: str; sides: tuple[Side, ...]; def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; def step(self, moves: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]) -> Step; def frame(self) -> Frame`
- **Invariants:**
  - `reset` returns float32 `(n_agents, obs_size)` per side, with every agent acting.
  - `moves[s]` is int64 `(n_agents,)` for discrete moves, or float32 `(n_agents, size)` for continuous ones. Rows of agents not acting are ignored.
  - The same seed and moves give identical `Step`s and `Frame`s (AD-11, AD-16).
- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a discrete move outside `[0, size)`, a non-finite continuous move, or the wrong number of sides or shape; `RuntimeError` for `step` before `reset` or after `done`
- **Boundary:** registry, evaluate, train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of three recorded games from seed 7: a 20-move Snake game, a 30-step 5-fish tank game, and a 30-step predator-prey game
- **Used by:** registry, evaluate, train, tournament, replay; PL-01, PL-03

### core.Agent: type
`class Agent(Protocol): name: str; def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray; def scores(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray | None`
- **Invariants:**
  - An agent plays one side: `obs` is that side's rows, and it returns one move per row, shaped as that side's `Env.step` entry. Each row is decided on its own (PG-06).
  - With `explore=False`, a value learner picks its highest score, ties by move order (straight, left, right), and a policy learner picks its most likely move.
  - `scores` is `(rows, len(score_names))`, or `None` for baselines.
- **Boundary:** registry, evaluate, train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of the scripted bot over a recorded 20-move game, a table learner with scores, a PPO policy over a recorded 5-fish game, and the scripted chaser over a recorded predator-prey game
- **Used by:** registry, colearn, evaluate, train, replay; PL-01, PL-03

### core.Learner: type
`class Learner(Agent, Protocol): updates: int; def observe(self, t: Transition, step: int, rng: np.random.Generator) -> None; def frozen(self) -> Agent; def settings(self) -> dict[str, int | float | str]; def save(self, path: Path) -> None; @classmethod def load(cls, path: Path) -> Learner`
- **Invariants:**
  - `observe` is the only place learning happens. It is called once per acting agent of the learner's side per step, in agent order.
  - `updates` counts the learning updates applied so far, starting at 0. For the table learner and the plain DQN rung, each `observe` is one update.
  - `act(explore=True)` is the training behaviour. Value learners explore at `Explore.rate(step)`; policy learners sample from the policy.
  - `frozen()` returns a copy that acts exactly as the learner does now and never changes (AD-17).
  - A loaded learner acts exactly like the saved one.
- **Errors:** `load` raises `FileNotFoundError` or `ValueError` for a missing or unreadable file
- **Boundary:** registry, colearn, train, store · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Learner/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of a table learner over 50 recorded transitions, a plain DQN rung, and a REINFORCE rung over a recorded 1-fish game; a PPO rung's frozen copy after 2 updates
- **Used by:** registry, colearn, train, store; PL-01

### core.Lineup: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Lineup: agents: tuple[Agent, ...]; learns: tuple[bool, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - One entry per side, in `Env.sides` order.
  - `agents[s]` plays side `s`. Where `learns[s]` is true, `agents[s]` is that side's `Learner`, and only those sides' learners observe (AD-17).
- **Boundary:** colearn, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Lineup/ · **Cases:** Snake's learner alone; rung 2 with the predator learning against the prey's own frozen learner; rung 3 with the prey learning against a pool copy; the prey control against the scripted chaser
- **Used by:** colearn, train; PL-01

### core.Schedule: type
`class Schedule(Protocol): learners: tuple[Learner | None, ...]; versions: int; def start_game(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Lineup; def current(self) -> Lineup`
- **Invariants:**
  - `learners[s]` is side `s`'s learner, or `None` for a side the run doesn't train. The sides with a learner are the run's learning sides.
  - `versions` is 10 for a predator-prey ladder rung and 0 otherwise (TOUR-01).
  - `start_game` is called at each training game's start; it is where opponents are drawn, with `rng` (COEV-04).
  - `current` is called after every step and reflects any switch due after that step's updates (COEV-03).
  - The same calls and the same learner updates give the same lineups.
- **Boundary:** registry, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Schedule/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of `solo` over 3 games, of `turns` across its first swap, and of `pool` over 12 recorded draws
- **Used by:** registry, train; PL-01

### core.Explore: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Explore: start: float; floor: float; decay_steps: int` with `def rate(self, step: int) -> float`
- **Invariants:** falls linearly from `start` at step 0 to `floor` at `decay_steps`, then stays; `1 ≥ start ≥ floor ≥ 0` (TAB-02, DQN-06)
- **Boundary:** tabular, dqn · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Explore/ · **Cases:** 1.0 → 0.05 over 100,000 steps, read at 0, 50,000 and 200,000
- **Used by:** tabular, dqn

## snake

### snake.SnakeEnv: The Snake game
`class SnakeEnv(Env): def __init__(self, reward: Literal["plain", "shaped"] = "plain") -> None`
- **Invariants:**
  - `name="snake"`, with one side, `snake`: 1 agent, discrete moves `straight, left, right`, and 11 obs in `Fact` order.
  - A fixed 10×10 board, drawn as cells.
  - `score` counts food whatever the reward (SHAPE-04).
  - Rewards per SNAKE-03–SNAKE-05, SNAKE-07, SHAPE-01 and SHAPE-02.
- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `ValueError` for an unknown `reward`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/snake.SnakeEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded game from seed 7, plain and shaped
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### snake.Fact: type
`class Fact(IntEnum): DANGER_STRAIGHT=0; DANGER_LEFT=1; DANGER_RIGHT=2; HEADING_UP=3; HEADING_DOWN=4; HEADING_LEFT=5; HEADING_RIGHT=6; FOOD_UP=7; FOOD_DOWN=8; FOOD_LEFT=9; FOOD_RIGHT=10`
- **Invariants:** each fact's index in an obs row; values 0.0 or 1.0; exactly one heading is 1
- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/snake.Fact/ · **Cases:** the start obs; the obs with the head at the top wall heading up
- **Used by:** baselines

## tank

### tank.TankEnv: The fish tank
`class TankEnv(Env): def __init__(self, fish: Literal[1, 5] = 1, view: Literal["short", "wide"] = "short") -> None`
- **Invariants:**
  - `name="tank"`, with one side, `fish`: `fish` agents, continuous moves `vx, vy` capped at 2.5, `score_names=("spread",)`, and 11 obs in `View` order.
  - Tank radius 10. Ball radius 0.5, moving at 2 units per second. Fish radius 0.25.
  - A step is 0.1 s, and the cap is 600 steps.
  - View radius 4 (`short`) or 20 (`wide`).
  - Step order per AD-13; rewards per TANK-09; `score` per TANK-10 (TANK-01–TANK-13).
- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `ValueError` for an unknown `fish` or `view`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tank.TankEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 1-fish game from seed 7; one of a 5-fish wide-view game with a removal and a bounce
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### tank.View: type
`class View(IntEnum): BALL_SEEN=0; BALL_X=1; BALL_Y=2; BALL_VX=3; BALL_VY=4; WALL_SEEN=5; WALL_X=6; WALL_Y=7; FISH_SEEN=8; FISH_X=9; FISH_Y=10`
- **Invariants:**
  - Each number's index in an obs row.
  - Positions, offsets and velocities are relative to the fish, in tank directions.
  - `*_SEEN` is 0.0 or 1.0, and an unseen item's numbers are 0 (TANK-11).
  - When two other fish are equally near, the one with the lower fish number counts as nearest.
- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tank.View/ · **Cases:** the short view with the ball 3 units away; the short view at the tank's centre; a 1-fish view
- **Used by:** baselines

## hunt

### hunt.HuntEnv: Predator and prey
`class HuntEnv(Env): def __init__(self) -> None`
- **Invariants:**
  - `name="hunt"`, with two sides, in this order: `predator` (1 agent, 11 obs in `PredatorView` order, capped at 3.0) and `prey` (5 agents, 14 obs in `PreyView` order, capped at 2.5). Both sides have continuous moves `vx, vy` and `score_names=("spread",)`.
  - Tank radius 10. Predator radius 0.5, prey radius 0.25. Catches within 0.75; pellets eaten within 0.5; always 10 pellets.
  - A step is 0.1 s, and the cap is 600 steps. Every view radius is 4 (HUNT-14).
  - Step order per AD-18; rewards per HUNT-09 and HUNT-10; `score` is `(catches, pellets eaten / 5)` (HUNT-11).
  - The predator's experience ends only when the game does (HUNT-01–HUNT-15).
- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/hunt.HuntEnv/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of a recorded game from seed 7 with a catch, a pellet respawn and a wall stop, and of one ending `all_caught`
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### hunt.PredatorView: type
`class PredatorView(IntEnum): WALL_SEEN=0; WALL_X=1; WALL_Y=2; PREY_SEEN=3; PREY_X=4; PREY_Y=5; PREY_VX=6; PREY_VY=7; PELLET_SEEN=8; PELLET_X=9; PELLET_Y=10`
- **Invariants:**
  - Each number's index in the predator's obs row (HUNT-13).
  - Positions and velocities are relative to the predator.
  - `*_SEEN` is 0.0 or 1.0, and an unseen item's numbers are 0.
  - When two prey or pellets are equally near, the lower-numbered one counts as nearest.
- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/hunt.PredatorView/ · **Cases:** the nearest prey 3 units away; every prey caught
- **Used by:** baselines

### hunt.PreyView: type
`class PreyView(IntEnum): PRED_SEEN=0; PRED_X=1; PRED_Y=2; PRED_VX=3; PRED_VY=4; WALL_SEEN=5; WALL_X=6; WALL_Y=7; PREY_SEEN=8; PREY_X=9; PREY_Y=10; PELLET_SEEN=11; PELLET_X=12; PELLET_Y=13`
- **Invariants:**
  - Each number's index in a prey's obs row (HUNT-12).
  - Positions and velocities are relative to the prey.
  - `*_SEEN` is 0.0 or 1.0, and an unseen item's numbers are 0.
  - When two other prey or pellets are equally near, the lower-numbered one counts as nearest.
- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/hunt.PreyView/ · **Cases:** the predator 3 units away with a pellet seen; the predator 5 units away
- **Used by:** baselines

## baselines

### baselines.RandomPlayer: Random player
`class RandomPlayer(Agent): name = "random"`
- **Invariants:** Snake; draws uniformly from the 3 moves with the given `rng`, whatever `explore` is; `scores` is `None` (BASE-01)
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomPlayer/ · **Cases:** 30 recorded moves from rng seed 3
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### baselines.ScriptedBot: Scripted bot
`class ScriptedBot(Agent): name = "scripted"`
- **Invariants:** Snake; reads only obs via `snake.Fact`; a safe move toward the food, else the closest safe move, else straight; ties by straight, left, right; `scores` is `None` (BASE-02, BASE-03)
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedBot/ · **Cases:** food left with left free; all moves fatal
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### baselines.RandomSwimmer: Random swimmer
`class RandomSwimmer(Agent): name = "random"; def __init__(self, max_speed: float = 2.5) -> None`
- **Invariants:**
  - For any fish: a tank fish or a prey at 2.5, the predator at 3.0.
  - For each row, a direction uniform on the circle and a speed uniform in `[0, max_speed]`, both from the given `rng`, whatever `explore` is.
  - `scores` is `None` (BASE-04).
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `max_speed` isn't positive
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomSwimmer/ · **Cases:** 30 recorded 5-fish moves from rng seed 3; 30 recorded predator moves at 3.0
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### baselines.FleeBot: Scripted fleeing bot
`class FleeBot(Agent): name = "scripted"`
- **Invariants:** the tank; reads only obs via `tank.View`; swims at 2.5 directly away from the ball's relative position when `BALL_SEEN`, else `(0, 0)`; `scores` is `None` (BASE-05, BASE-06)
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.FleeBot/ · **Cases:** ball seen at (1, 0); ball unseen
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### baselines.ChaseBot: Scripted chaser
`class ChaseBot(Agent): name = "scripted"`
- **Invariants:**
  - Predator-prey's predator. It reads only obs, via `hunt.PredatorView`.
  - When `PREY_SEEN`, it swims at 3.0 directly toward the nearest prey's relative position; otherwise `(0, 0)`.
  - `scores` is `None` (BASE-08, BASE-09).
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ChaseBot/ · **Cases:** prey seen at (1, 0); no prey seen
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### baselines.ForageBot: Scripted forager
`class ForageBot(Agent): name = "scripted"`
- **Invariants:**
  - Predator-prey's prey. It reads only obs, via `hunt.PreyView`.
  - When `PRED_SEEN`, it swims at 2.5 directly away from the predator. Otherwise, when `PELLET_SEEN`, it swims at 2.5 toward the nearest pellet. Otherwise `(0, 0)`.
  - `scores` is `None` (BASE-10–BASE-12).
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ForageBot/ · **Cases:** predator at (0, 2) with a pellet seen; predator unseen, pellet at (−3, 0); nothing seen
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

## tabular

### tabular.TabularSettings: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TabularSettings: lr: float; gamma: float; explore: Explore`
- **Invariants:** `0 < lr ≤ 1`; `0 ≤ gamma < 1`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tabular.TabularSettings/ · **Cases:** lr 0.1, gamma 0.9, explore 1.0 → 0.01 over 50,000
- **Used by:** registry

### tabular.TabularQ: Table-based Q-learner
`class TabularQ(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: TabularSettings, n_obs: int = 11, n_actions: int = 3) -> None`
- **Invariants:**
  - Discrete moves only. One row of scores per distinct obs, starting at 0.
  - `observe` applies `Q ← Q + lr·(r + gamma·max Q(next) − Q)`, with no next term when `done`, and counts it as an update (TAB-01).
  - Saves `.npz`.
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the obs length isn't `n_obs`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tabular.TabularQ/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed update; a game-ending update
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

## dqn

### dqn.DQNSettings: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class DQNSettings: rung: Literal["plain", "replay", "target", "double"]; lr: float; gamma: float; hidden: tuple[int, ...]; explore: Explore; batch_size: int; buffer_size: int; target_every: int`
- **Invariants:** each rung adds one piece to the one before (AD-03); `plain` ignores `batch_size`, `buffer_size`, `target_every`; `hidden` defaults to `(64, 64)`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/dqn.DQNSettings/ · **Cases:** one per rung, all other fields equal
- **Used by:** registry

### dqn.DQNAgent: DQN learner
`class DQNAgent(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: DQNSettings, n_obs: int, n_actions: int, torch_seed: int) -> None`
- **Invariants:**
  - Discrete moves only, CPU only.
  - The target is `r + gamma·Q′(next, a*)`, with no next term when `done`. `Q′` is the learner itself for `plain` and `replay`, and the frozen copy for `target` and `double`. `a*` is `Q′`'s argmax, except under `double`, where it is the learner's.
  - Batches wait for a full store, and the copy refreshes every `target_every` updates (DQN-01–DQN-04).
  - `updates` counts gradient steps.
  - Saves `.pt`.
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `buffer_size < batch_size` from `replay` up
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/dqn.DQNAgent/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed target per rung
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

## pg

### pg.PGSettings: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PGSettings: rung: Literal["reinforce", "baseline", "actor_critic", "ppo"]; lr: float; critic_lr: float; gamma: float; hidden: tuple[int, ...]; init_log_spread: float; steps_per_update: int; passes: int; minibatch: int; clip: float`
- **Invariants:**
  - Each rung adds one piece to the one before (AD-12).
  - `reinforce` ignores `critic_lr`, `steps_per_update`, `passes`, `minibatch` and `clip`. `baseline` adds `critic_lr`, `actor_critic` adds `steps_per_update`, and `ppo` adds `passes`, `minibatch` and `clip`.
  - `hidden` defaults to `(64, 64)`; `0 ≤ gamma < 1`; `0 < clip < 1`.
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGSettings/ · **Cases:** one per rung, all other fields equal
- **Used by:** registry

### pg.PGAgent: Policy-gradient learner
`class PGAgent(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: PGSettings, n_obs: int, action_size: int, torch_seed: int) -> None`
- **Invariants:**
  - Continuous moves only, CPU only. A diagonal Gaussian policy per obs row, its log spread clamped to [−5, 1].
  - `act(explore=True)` samples with `rng`; `explore=False` returns the mean (PG-07). `scores` is the mean of the two spreads. It learns from the sampled move.
  - `reinforce` and `baseline` update once every agent's experience has ended; `actor_critic` and `ppo` update every `steps_per_update` steps. Weights follow PG-01–PG-04, with no next term when `done`.
  - Every fish of its side trains the one policy (PG-06). Each predator-prey side is its own `PGAgent` (COEV-01).
  - Every update goes through the `pg` functions below. `last_update: UpdateLog | None` holds the latest update, and is `None` before the first (AD-15).
  - `updates` counts updates. `frozen()` copies the policy alone.
  - Saves `.pt`.
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `minibatch > steps_per_update` under `ppo`
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGAgent/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed update per rung; a PPO move already past the clip; a 14-obs prey policy's update over a 5-prey batch with a capture
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

### pg.UpdateLog: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class UpdateLog: rung: str; weights: torch.Tensor; log_probs: torch.Tensor; values: torch.Tensor | None; critic_loss: tuple[float, float] | None; minibatches: tuple[torch.Tensor, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - One entry per move the update learned from, in fish order then time order (AD-15).
  - `weights` come from `move_weights`.
  - `log_probs` come from `gaussian_log_prob` at the sampled move, before training changes the policy.
  - `values` and `critic_loss` (squared error to the targets, before and after the critic's step) are `None` under `reinforce`.
  - `minibatches` is `minibatch_plan`'s output under `ppo`, and one index tensor covering every move otherwise.
- **Boundary:** tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.UpdateLog/ · **Cases:** a REINFORCE update over a 12-step 1-fish game; a PPO update over a 5-fish batch
- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29

### pg.discounted_returns: Discounted returns
`def discounted_returns(rewards: torch.Tensor, gamma: float) -> torch.Tensor`
- **Invariants:** `rewards` float32 `(T,)` is one fish's moves in time order, its experience ending at the last move; returns `G_t = Σ_k gamma^k r_{t+k}`, same shape and dtype (PG-01)
- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29

### pg.move_weights: Each rung's weight per move
`def move_weights(rung: str, rewards: torch.Tensor, done: torch.Tensor, values: torch.Tensor | None, next_values: torch.Tensor | None, gamma: float) -> torch.Tensor`
- **Invariants:**
  - Inputs are `(T,)`: one fish's moves in time order.
  - `reinforce`: `G_t`. `baseline`: `G_t − V(s_t)`. `actor_critic` and `ppo`: `r_t + gamma·V(s_{t+1}) − V(s_t)`, with no next term where `done` (PG-01–PG-04).
  - The weights are never part of the gradient.
- **Errors:** `ValueError` for an unknown rung, or `values`/`next_values` missing where the rung needs them
- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29

### pg.gaussian_log_prob: Log-probability of a swim
`def gaussian_log_prob(mean: torch.Tensor, log_spread: torch.Tensor, action: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor`
- **Invariants:** inputs `(N, size)`, `log_spread` already clamped to [−5, 1]; returns `(N,)` `Σ_i [−½((a_i − μ_i)/σ_i)² − log σ_i − ½ log 2π]`, differentiable in `mean` and `log_spread`
- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29

### pg.ppo_objective: Clipped objective per move
`def ppo_objective(log_prob: torch.Tensor, old_log_prob: torch.Tensor, weight: torch.Tensor, clip: float) -> torch.Tensor`
- **Invariants:**
  - Inputs are `(N,)`, and `ρ = exp(log_prob − old_log_prob)`.
  - Returns `(N,)` `min(ρ·A, clip(ρ, 1 − clip, 1 + clip)·A)`.
  - A move with `ρ > 1 + clip` and `A > 0`, or with `ρ < 1 − clip` and `A < 0`, has a gradient of exactly 0 (PG-04).
- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29

### pg.minibatch_plan: Passes over a batch
`def minibatch_plan(n_moves: int, passes: int, minibatch: int, gen: torch.Generator) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - `passes` passes, in order. Each is a fresh permutation of `range(n_moves)` from `gen`, cut into minibatches of `minibatch`; the last may be shorter.
  - Every move appears once per pass (PG-04).
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if any argument is below 1
- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29

## colearn

### colearn.Pool: type
`class Pool: def __init__(self, start: Agent, size: int = 10) -> None; copies: tuple[Agent, ...]; def add(self, agent: Agent) -> None; def draw(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Agent`
- **Invariants:**
  - A pool starts holding `start` alone.
  - The first `add` replaces `start`, and after that the pool keeps the latest `size` copies, oldest first.
  - `draw` picks uniformly from `copies`, the latest included (COEV-04 and its edge case).
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `size` is below 1
- **Boundary:** tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/colearn.Pool/ · **Cases:** a fresh pool; 11 copies added; 10,000 draws from 10 copies with rng seed 3
- **Used by:** colearn

### colearn.make_schedule: Build a schedule
`def make_schedule(kind: Literal["solo", "together", "turns", "pool", "control"], learners: tuple[Learner | None, ...], bots: tuple[Agent | None, ...], *, swap_every: int = 20, copy_every: int = 20, pool_size: int = 10) -> Schedule`
- **Invariants:**
  - `learners` and `bots` have one entry per side, and each side has exactly one of the two.
  - `solo`: one side, always learning.
  - `together`: both sides learn every step (COEV-02).
  - `turns`: side 0 learns first. A side stops learning straight after its `swap_every`-th update of the turn, and the other side starts at the next `start_game` (COEV-03).
  - `pool`: as `turns`, but the side not learning is played by a draw from its `Pool` at each `start_game`. Each side adds `frozen()` every `copy_every` of its updates (COEV-04).
  - `control`: the side with a learner always learns, against the other side's bot (COEV-05).
  - `versions` is 10 for `together`, `turns` and `pool`, and 0 otherwise (AD-17).
- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a kind that doesn't fit the given sides, or an interval below 1
- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/colearn.make_schedule/ · **Cases:** `solo` for Snake; `together`; `turns` over 45 recorded predator updates; `pool` over 12 games; the predator `control`
- **Used by:** registry; PL-01

## registry

### registry.UnknownName: type
`class UnknownName(ValueError): kind: str; name: str; choices: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `kind` is `env`, `setup`, `match`, `side`, `learner` or `run`; `choices` lists the valid names for that environment; the message lists them (RUN-01 edge case)
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.UnknownName/ · **Cases:** an unknown reward; a Snake learner asked for in the tank; an unknown predator-prey match
- **Used by:** cli, train

### registry.make_env: Build an environment
`def make_env(env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str]) -> Env`
- **Invariants:**
  - Each call builds a fresh environment. `env` is in `ENVS`: `snake`, `tank` or `hunt`, and tests register a second.
  - Snake's `setup` is `{"reward": "plain" | "shaped" | "none"}`; `none` plays plain, for baselines.
  - The tank's `setup` is `{"fish": 1 | 5, "view": "short" | "wide"}`.
  - Predator-prey's `setup` is either `{"match": "rung-1" | "rung-2" | "rung-3" | "control-predator" | "control-prey"}` for learners or `{"side": "predator" | "prey"}` for baselines, and every one builds the same `HuntEnv`.
- **Errors:** `UnknownName` for an unknown environment, setup key or setup value
- **Boundary:** train, tournament, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_env/ · **Cases:** `snake`/`shaped`; `tank`/5 fish/`wide`; `hunt`/`rung-3`; an unknown reward; `reward` given to the tank
- **Used by:** train, tournament, replay; PL-01, PL-03

### registry.default_params: A run's full settings
`def default_params(learner: str, *, env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str]) -> dict[str, int | float | str]`
- **Invariants:**
  - Returns every setting the run uses, flattened as `RunSettings.params` (`lr`, `explore.start`, …); `{}` for `random` and `scripted` (AD-03, AD-10, AD-12).
  - Predator-prey's `pg-ppo` prefixes each trained side's settings with its side name (`predator.lr`, `prey.clip`, …). It adds `colearn.swap_every` from rung 2 and `colearn.copy_every` and `colearn.pool_size` on rung 3 (AD-17).
  - Rule for comparisons: the four `dqn-*` names differ only in `rung`, and so do the four `pg-*` names. Predator-prey's rungs differ only in those added settings, and each control in which side has settings.
- **Errors:** `UnknownName`
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.default_params/ · **Cases:** `tabular`; `dqn-double`; `pg-ppo` on the tank; `pg-ppo` on `rung-3`; `pg-ppo` on `control-prey`; `scripted`
- **Used by:** cli

### registry.make_agents: Build a run's agents from settings
`def make_agents(learner: str, params: Mapping[str, int | float | str], *, env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str], init_seeds: tuple[int, ...]) -> tuple[Agent | None, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - Returns one entry per side, each a side the run trains or records, or `None` for a side played only by its opponents.
  - Snake's learners are `tabular`, `dqn-plain`, `dqn-replay`, `dqn-target` and `dqn-double`. The tank's are `pg-reinforce`, `pg-baseline`, `pg-actor-critic` and `pg-ppo`.
  - Predator-prey's only learner is `pg-ppo`: both sides on a rung, and the named side on a control (COEV-01, COEV-05).
  - `random` and `scripted` build the baselines of the environment, or of the side given as `setup["side"]`.
  - `params` holds every `default_params` key (AD-10), and learners come back as a `Learner` built from that side's `init_seeds` entry.
- **Errors:** `UnknownName`, its choices the environment's own names, when `learner` is unknown or belongs to another environment; `ValueError` for invalid or missing params
- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_agents/ · **Cases:** `tabular` with defaults; `pg-ppo` on the tank with defaults; `pg-ppo` on `rung-1`; `pg-ppo` on `control-predator`; `scripted` on side `prey`; `dqn-triple`; `dqn-plain` for the tank
- **Used by:** train; PL-01

### registry.make_schedule: Build a run's schedule
`def make_schedule(env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str], agents: tuple[Agent | None, ...], params: Mapping[str, int | float | str]) -> Schedule`
- **Invariants:**
  - Snake and the tank get `solo`.
  - Predator-prey's `rung-1`, `rung-2` and `rung-3` get `together`, `turns` and `pool`. `control-predator` and `control-prey` get `control`, with the other side's scripted bot.
  - Intervals come from `params` (AD-17).
  - A baseline record gets no schedule; `train` only tests it.
- **Errors:** `UnknownName` for an unknown match
- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_schedule/ · **Cases:** Snake's `solo`; `rung-2`; `control-prey`
- **Used by:** train; PL-01

### registry.test_lineup: Each side's fixed test opponents
`def test_lineup(env: str, side: int, agent: Agent) -> tuple[Agent, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - `agent` plays side `side`, and every other side is played by that side's scripted bot: the predator by the scripted chaser, the prey by the scripted forager (EVAL-01, BASE-07).
  - A one-side environment returns `(agent,)`.
- **Errors:** `IndexError` for a side the environment doesn't have
- **Boundary:** train, tournament, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.test_lineup/ · **Cases:** Snake's learner; a learning predator; the random prey
- **Used by:** train, replay; PL-01, PL-03

### registry.load_agent: Load a saved agent
`def load_agent(record: RunRecord, *, side: str | None = None, version: int | None = None) -> Agent`
- **Invariants:**
  - `side` defaults to the run's only trained or recorded side.
  - A baseline record rebuilds that side's baseline. A learner record loads `agent-<side>`, or `versions/<side>-<version>` when `version` is given (TOUR-01).
- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the missing file; `ValueError` if `side` is needed but not given, or `version` is given for a run with no versions
- **Boundary:** replay, tournament · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.load_agent/ · **Cases:** a saved table learner; a scripted-bot record; a saved PPO policy; a rung-2 run's prey version 4
- **Used by:** replay, tournament; PL-03

## evaluate

### evaluate.TestGame: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TestGame: seed: int; side: int; moves: tuple[tuple[np.ndarray, ...], ...]; scores: tuple[tuple[np.ndarray | None, ...], ...]; score_after: tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]; ending: str; score: tuple[float, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - `side` is the tested side.
  - There is one `moves`, `scores` and `score_after` entry per step, each with one item per side (AD-16).
  - A `moves` item is that side's `Env.step` input, with rows of agents not acting set to 0.
  - A `scores` item is `(n_agents, len(score_names))`, with rows of agents not acting NaN, or `None` for a side played by a baseline.
  - `score == score_after[-1]`.
- **Boundary:** train, tournament, store, replay, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.TestGame/ · **Cases:** a table-learner game ending on a wall; a scripted-bot game ending starved; a one-move game; a 5-fish PPO game reaching the cap; a learning predator's game against the scripted forager ending `all_caught`
- **Used by:** train, tournament, store, replay, compare

### evaluate.TestResult: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TestResult: side: int; games: tuple[TestGame, ...]; score: float`
- **Invariants:** one game per seed played, in seed order; `score` is the mean of the games' `score[side]` (EVAL-02)
- **Boundary:** train, tournament · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.TestResult/ · **Cases:** 100 recorded scripted-bot games; 100 recorded flee-bot games; 100 recorded prey games against the scripted chaser
- **Used by:** train, tournament

### evaluate.play_game: Play one greedy game
`def play_game(env: Env, lineup: tuple[Agent, ...], seed: int, rng: np.random.Generator, *, side: int = 0) -> TestGame`
- **Invariants:** `act(explore=False)` on every side's acting rows at every step; `rng` reaches only agents that ignore `explore`; same inputs → same game
- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.play_game/ · **Cases:** the scripted bot on seed 7; the flee bot with 5 fish on seed 7; the scripted chaser against the scripted forager on seed 7
- **Used by:** replay, train, tournament; PL-01, PL-03

### evaluate.test_agent: Score a side on the test games
`def test_agent(env: Env, lineup: tuple[Agent, ...], *, side: int = 0, seeds: Sequence[int] = range(100), rng_seed: int = 0) -> TestResult`
- **Invariants:**
  - Plays `seeds` of the given environment setup. Each is a test seed in 0–99, never used in training (EVAL-01).
  - Scores side `side` by its `Step.score`, never the training reward (SHAPE-04).
- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a seed outside 0–99
- **Boundary:** train, tournament · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.test_agent/ · **Cases:** the scripted bot's 100 recorded games; the flee bot's 100 recorded 1-fish games; the random predator's 100 recorded games against the scripted forager
- **Used by:** train, tournament; PL-01

## store

### store.RunSettings: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunSettings: env: str; learner: str; setup: Mapping[str, int | str]; seed: int; train_steps: int; params: Mapping[str, int | float | str]; curve_points: int = 20; max_minutes: float = 60.0`
- **Invariants:**
  - `setup` is as `registry.make_env` takes it: Snake's names its reward (SHAPE-03), the tank's its fish count and view, and predator-prey's its match or a baseline's side.
  - `params` holds every setting of the run, flattened (`explore.start`, `predator.lr`, …): `default_params` with the user's overrides (AD-10).
  - `train_steps` counts environment steps.
  - Saved with library versions, enough to recreate the run (RUN-03).
  - A baseline has `train_steps=0`; Snake's has reward `none`.
- **Boundary:** cli, train, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.RunSettings/ · **Cases:** a tabular plain run; a double-DQN shaped run; a scripted-bot record; a 5-fish wide-view PPO run; a 1-fish flee-bot record; a rung-3 run; a random-prey record
- **Used by:** cli, train, compare

### store.CurvePoint: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePoint: steps: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; elapsed_s: float`
- **Invariants:** `steps` strictly increasing within a run; `scores` holds one `TestResult.score` per learning side, keyed by side name (RUN-04, EVAL-03)
- **Boundary:** train, compare, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.CurvePoint/ · **Cases:** the 20 points of a recorded tabular run; 3 points of a rung-1 run
- **Used by:** train, compare, cli

### store.RunRecord: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: run_id: str; path: Path; settings: RunSettings; status: Literal["complete", "incomplete", "over_time"]; curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]; missing: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - `complete` means all the run's evidence is present: settings, curve, each trained side's agent, any versions, and the test games (RUN-02, TOUR-01).
  - `missing` names the absent files.
  - A folder left `running` reads as `incomplete`.
- **Boundary:** train, tournament, compare, replay, registry, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a complete run; an over-time run; a run missing its test games after a disk error; a complete tank run; a complete rung-2 run; a rung-3 run missing prey version 7
- **Used by:** train, tournament, compare, replay, registry, cli

### store.StagedRun: type
`class StagedRun(Protocol): run_id: str; path: Path; def add_point(self, point: CurvePoint) -> None; def add_version(self, side: str, k: int, learner: Learner) -> None; def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete", "over_time"], *, agents: Mapping[str, Agent], games: Sequence[TestGame]) -> RunRecord`
- **Invariants:**
  - `add_point` writes at once.
  - `add_version` saves `versions/<side>-<k>.pt`, with `k` from 1 to 10 (TOUR-01).
  - `finish` writes each trained side's agent and the games (moves as exact JSON numbers, AD-06), then `status.json` last.
  - Every file is written by rename. A failed write gives `incomplete`, with `missing` set.
- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.StagedRun/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of a 3-point run finished complete, of one finished incomplete after an `OSError`, and of a rung-1 run with 2 versions per side
- **Used by:** train; PL-01

### store.begin_run: Start a run folder
`def begin_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> StagedRun`
- **Invariants:**
  - Creates a new folder `<env>/<learner>/<setup>/seed-<n>/<UTC timestamp>-<id>/` every time, never reused (RUN-05).
  - `<setup>` is the reward in Snake, `fish-<n>-<view>` in the tank, and the match or the baseline's side in predator-prey.
  - Writes `settings.json` and a `running` status.
- **Errors:** `OSError` if the folder can't be created
- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.begin_run/ · **Cases:** two runs with identical settings; a tank run's folder name; a predator-prey control's folder name
- **Used by:** train; PL-01

### store.list_runs: Find runs
`def list_runs(*, root: Path = Path("runs"), env: str | None = None, learner: str | None = None, setup: str | None = None, complete_only: bool = False) -> list[RunRecord]`
- **Invariants:** `setup` is the folder label; sorted by environment, learner, setup, seed, run id; an unreadable folder comes back `incomplete` with `missing` set, never skipped
- **Boundary:** tournament, compare, replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.list_runs/ · **Cases:** a runs folder with one complete, one over-time and one broken run; one with Snake, tank and predator-prey runs
- **Used by:** tournament, compare, replay, cli; PL-02, PL-03

### store.load_test_games: Read a run's test games
`def load_test_games(record: RunRecord) -> list[TestGame]`
- **Invariants:** each tested side's games in side order, then seeds 0–99 in order, exactly as saved, moves bit for bit (AD-06)
- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run and file
- **Boundary:** replay, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.load_test_games/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake run's 100 games; a complete 5-fish run's 100 games; a complete rung-1 run's 200 games
- **Used by:** replay, compare; PL-02, PL-03

### store.TournamentGrid: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TournamentGrid: run_id: str; setup: str; seed: int; seeds: tuple[int, ...]; catches: np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:**
  - `catches` is float `(10, 10)`, predator version `i` by prey version `j`, both counted from 1 as rows and columns 0–9. Each cell is the mean predator score (HUNT-11) over `seeds`.
  - `seeds` are test seeds 0–19 (TOUR-02, AD-19).
- **Boundary:** tournament, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.TournamentGrid/ · **Cases:** a rung-1 grid that cycles; a rung-3 grid rising with the predator's version
- **Used by:** tournament, compare; PL-02

### store.save_tournament: Save a run's tournament
`def save_tournament(record: RunRecord, grid: TournamentGrid, *, root: Path = Path("tournaments")) -> Path`
- **Invariants:** writes `<env>/<learner>/<setup>/seed-<n>/<run id>.json` by rename, once (AD-19)
- **Errors:** `FileExistsError` if the run already has a grid; `OSError` on a failed write
- **Boundary:** tournament · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.save_tournament/ · **Cases:** a first save; a second save of the same run
- **Used by:** tournament

### store.load_tournament: Read a run's tournament
`def load_tournament(record: RunRecord, *, root: Path = Path("tournaments")) -> TournamentGrid | None`
- **Invariants:** `None` when the run has no grid; otherwise the grid exactly as saved
- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the file if it is unreadable
- **Boundary:** tournament, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.load_tournament/ · **Cases:** a saved grid; a run with none
- **Used by:** tournament, compare; PL-02

## train

### train.train_run: One training run
`def train_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs"), progress: Callable[[CurvePoint], None] | None = None) -> RunRecord`
- **Invariants:**
  - Trains one environment setup from one seed, with the agents of `registry.make_agents` and the schedule of `registry.make_schedule` (RUN-01, AD-17).
  - Each training game starts with `start_game`. On each step, every side's lineup agent acts on its side's acting rows with `explore=True`. Each side that learns observes once per acting agent, in agent order (PG-06, AD-11), and `current` follows the step.
  - At each of `curve_points` evenly spaced test rounds, each learning side is tested against `registry.test_lineup`, and the point goes to `progress` (RUN-04).
  - With `versions` 10, each learning side is saved at every second point (TOUR-01).
  - A baseline plays its test lineup once.
  - Stops at `max_minutes` as `over_time` (RUN-07); Ctrl-C ends `incomplete` (RUN-06).
  - The same settings give the same curve (RUN-03).
- **Errors:** `UnknownName` before any folder exists; a save failure returns an `incomplete` record
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train_run/ · **Cases:** a recorded 3-point tabular run; an over-time run; a recorded 3-point 5-fish PPO run; a recorded 4-point rung-2 run with 2 versions per side
- **Used by:** cli; PL-01

## tournament

### tournament.play_tournament: Play one run's tournament
`def play_tournament(record: RunRecord, *, seeds: Sequence[int] = range(20)) -> TournamentGrid`
- **Invariants:**
  - `record` is a complete predator-prey ladder run.
  - Each of its 10 predator versions plays each of its 10 prey versions, both with `explore=False`, on `seeds`.
  - Each cell is the mean predator score (TOUR-02).
  - The same record and seeds give the same grid.
- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a run that isn't a complete ladder run; `FileNotFoundError` naming a missing version
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tournament.play_tournament/ · **Cases:** a recorded rung-1 run whose versions are random and scripted stand-ins; a control run
- **Used by:** tournament

### tournament.run_tournaments: Play every missing tournament
`def run_tournaments(*, runs: Path = Path("runs"), out: Path = Path("tournaments"), progress: Callable[[str], None] | None = None) -> tuple[TournamentGrid, ...]`
- **Invariants:** plays and saves a grid for every complete predator-prey ladder run without one, in `list_runs` order, passing each run id to `progress`; returns the grids it made; never replays a saved grid (AD-19)
- **Errors:** a run that fails is reported through `progress` and left without a grid, never raised
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tournament.run_tournaments/ · **Cases:** a fake over two ladder runs, one already played
- **Used by:** cli

## compare

### compare.Comparison: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: comparison_id: str; env: str; kind: Literal["baseline", "ladder", "tournament"]; side: str | None; a: str; b: str; outcome: Literal["beats", "ties", "loses", "better", "worse", "no_difference", "trend", "no_trend"]; test: str; p_adjusted: float; note: str | None; explanation: str | None`
- **Invariants:**
  - `a` and `b` each name a learner and setup, or a baseline and setup. A tournament names its rung in both.
  - `side` names the compared side in a multi-side environment.
  - A tournament's `p_adjusted` is the larger of its two trend tests' (AD-19).
  - `note` is set on a predator-prey control comparison: "the control trained against <bot>, its test opponent" (EVAL-08).
  - A result is explained exactly when `explanation` is non-empty (EVAL-06).
  - `comparison_id` names the explanation file under `reports/<env>/explanations/`.
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Comparison/ · **Cases:** an explained tie; an unexplained no-difference; a beat; a tank ladder step; a predator control step with its note; an unexplained no-trend tournament
- **Used by:** cli

### compare.Report: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Report: env: str; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; waiting: tuple[str, ...]; done: bool; not_done: tuple[str, ...]; path: Path`
- **Invariants:**
  - There is one report per environment (AD-14).
  - `waiting` names each comparison or tournament chart not made, and the seeds, settings or grids blocking it (EVAL-04, TOUR-03).
  - `done` holds exactly when EVAL-07 holds for `env`; otherwise `not_done` names each gap.
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Report/ · **Cases:** Snake done with an explained tie; waiting on two seeds; the tank not done because short-view PPO loses to the flee bot; predator-prey not done because rung 3's no-trend is unexplained
- **Used by:** cli

### compare.Chart: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Chart: env: str; kind: Literal["curve", "tournament"]; learner: str; setup: str; side: str | None; path: Path`
- **Invariants:**
  - A `curve` chart is drawn for each learner, setup and learning side with complete runs, with the baseline lines from the same setup and side (BASE-07, EVAL-03).
  - A `tournament` chart is drawn for each rung with 5 grids, and has `side` set to `None` (TOUR-03).
  - `path` is a PNG under `reports/<env>/`, linked from that report (AD-09).
- **Boundary:** compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Chart/ · **Cases:** a tabular plain chart; a double-DQN shaped chart; a 5-fish wide-view PPO chart; a rung-1 prey chart; a rung-3 tournament chart
- **Used by:** compare; PL-02

### compare.build_report: Make every comparison
`def build_report(*, runs: Path = Path("runs"), tournaments: Path = Path("tournaments"), out: Path = Path("reports")) -> tuple[Report, ...]`
- **Invariants:**
  - Writes one report per environment with runs (AD-14). Every test goes through the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill on complete runs only, corrected within that report (AD-08).
  - EVAL-04 compares each learner setup's learning side with that side's baselines on the same setup (BASE-07).
  - Snake:
    - EVAL-05 steps: `tabular`→`dqn-plain`→`dqn-replay`→`dqn-target`→`dqn-double` on `plain`, then `dqn-double` `plain`→`shaped`.
    - Top learner: `dqn-double` on `plain`.
  - The tank:
    - EVAL-05 steps: `pg-reinforce`→`pg-baseline`→`pg-actor-critic`→`pg-ppo` on `fish-1-short`, then `pg-ppo` `fish-1-short`→`fish-5-short`→`fish-5-wide`.
    - Top learner: `pg-ppo` on `fish-5-short`.
  - Predator-prey, 28 tests:
    - EVAL-04: each side's `control-<side>`, `rung-1`, `rung-2` and `rung-3` against the random and scripted bots of `predator` or `prey`.
    - EVAL-05, per side: `control-<side>`→`rung-1`→`rung-2`→`rung-3`, with EVAL-08's note on the control steps.
    - TOUR-04: two trend tests per rung.
    - Top learners: both sides of `rung-3`, plus every tournament (EVAL-07).
  - A pair whose settings differ beyond the added piece is refused (DQN-05, SHAPE-05, PG-05, COEV-06).
  - Writes charts per the `dataviz` skill (EVAL-03, TOUR-03).
- **Errors:** a refused or short pair, or a rung missing grids, goes to `waiting`, never raised
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.build_report/ · **Cases:** 5 seeds of two Snake rungs with both baselines; 5 seeds of two tank rungs with both tank baselines; 5 seeds of `rung-1` and `rung-2` with both sides' baselines and their 10 grids
- **Used by:** cli; PL-02

## replay

### replay.ReplayGame: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ReplayGame: title: str; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]; moves: tuple[tuple[np.ndarray, ...], ...]; scores: tuple[tuple[np.ndarray | None, ...], ...]; score_after: tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]; ending: str; sides: tuple[Side, ...]`
- **Invariants:** one more frame than moves (the start frame); `moves`, `scores` and `score_after` as in `TestGame`, one item per side of `sides`
- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.ReplayGame/ · **Cases:** a 12-move table-learner game; a baseline game; a one-move game; a 40-step 5-fish PPO game with a removal; a 40-step predator-prey game with a learning predator against the scripted forager
- **Used by:** replay; PL-03

### replay.ShowLog: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ShowLog: frames_shown: int; pauses: int; steps: int; final_speed: int; closed_early: bool`
- **Invariants:** `frames_shown` ≤ the game's frames; `final_speed` in 1–30; `closed_early` when the window closed before the last frame
- **Boundary:** replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.ShowLog/ · **Cases:** the recorded event script (pause, step, speed up) on a 12-move game; a window closed at the start frame
- **Used by:** replay, cli; PL-03

### replay.run_replay: Find a run and watch one of its games
`def run_replay(run_id: str, *, index: int | None = None, seed: int | None = None, side: str | None = None, speed: int = 10, runs: Path = Path("runs")) -> ShowLog`
- **Invariants:** finds `run_id` among `store.list_runs`; passes `index`, `seed` and `side` through to `open_game` unchanged, then `show` (REPLAY-01, REPLAY-07)
- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming a missing run; `ValueError` if both `index` and `seed` are given; as `open_game` and `show`
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.run_replay/ · **Cases:** a fake watching game 0 of a table run; an unknown run id
- **Used by:** cli; PL-03

### replay.run_match: Pair saved agents and watch a fresh game
`def run_match(env: str, picks: Mapping[str, str], *, seed: int, speed: int = 10, runs: Path = Path("runs")) -> ShowLog`
- **Invariants:** `picks` maps each side name to a run id or `scripted`; finds each run among `store.list_runs`, then `open_match` and `show` (REPLAY-11)
- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming a missing run; `ValueError` for a side missing from `picks`; as `open_match` and `show`
- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.run_match/ · **Cases:** a fake pairing a rung-3 predator with a rung-1 run's prey; the prey control against `scripted`
- **Used by:** cli; PL-03

### replay.open_game: Rebuild a game to watch
`def open_game(record: RunRecord, *, index: int | None = None, seed: int | None = None, side: str | None = None) -> ReplayGame`
- **Invariants:**
  - Takes at most one of `index` and `seed`; with neither, it opens saved game 0.
  - `index` rebuilds a saved test game from its seed and moves (REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06).
  - `seed` has the agent play greedily on a fresh seed of the run's setup, in that side's test lineup (REPLAY-07).
  - `side` picks which tested side's games `index` counts in, and which side plays a fresh seed. It defaults to the run's first learning or recorded side.
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if both `index` and `seed` are given, or for a side the run didn't test; `FileNotFoundError` or `IndexError` naming the missing item; `class ReplayMismatch(RuntimeError): move: int; expected: str; got: str`, at the first step whose game score or ending differs
- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_game/ · **Cases:** saved game 0 of a table run; neither `index` nor `seed` (saved game 0); seed 12345; a tampered saved game; saved game 3 of a 5-fish run; saved prey game 5 of a rung-2 run
- **Used by:** replay; PL-03

### replay.open_match: Rebuild a fresh pairing to watch
`def open_match(env: str, picks: Mapping[str, RunRecord | None], *, seed: int) -> ReplayGame`
- **Invariants:**
  - One pick per side. A record plays its saved agent for that side; `None` plays that side's scripted bot.
  - The game is played greedily from `seed`, and the same picks and seed give the same game (REPLAY-11).
- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a side missing from `picks`, or a record with no agent for that side; `FileNotFoundError` naming a missing agent file
- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_match/ · **Cases:** two runs' predator and prey on seed 12345, played twice; a saved predator against the scripted forager
- **Used by:** replay; PL-03

### replay.show: Watch a replay
`def show(game: ReplayGame, *, speed: int = 10) -> ShowLog`
- **Invariants:**
  - Opens a pygame-ce window drawing each frame's shapes and labels, the step number and the ending (REPLAY-02, REPLAY-08, REPLAY-10).
  - For each side, by its `actions` (REPLAY-03, REPLAY-09):
    - discrete moves: one bar per `score_names` entry, with the chosen move marked
    - continuous moves: an arrow from each acting agent's shape along its move, with its spread
    - "n/a" where the side's `scores` is `None`
  - Space pauses and resumes; → steps while paused; ↑/↓ set 1–30 steps per second (REPLAY-04, REPLAY-05).
  - Returns what was shown when the window closes.
- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `speed` is outside 1–30
- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.show/ · **Cases:** a recorded event script (pause, step, speed up) on a 12-move game; the same script on a 5-fish game; on a predator-prey game with a scripted side
- **Used by:** replay; PL-03

## cli

### cli.main: The rllab command
`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
- **Invariants:**
  - `rllab train [--env snake|tank|hunt] --learner L [--reward R | --fish F --view V | --match M | --side S] --seed N [--steps S]`. `--env` defaults to `snake`, and a baseline name records a baseline.
  - `rllab tournament` runs `tournament.run_tournaments`.
  - `rllab compare` builds every environment's report.
  - `rllab replay RUN_ID [--game I | --seed N] [--side S] [--speed V]` calls `replay.run_replay`.
  - `rllab replay --env hunt --predator RUN_ID|scripted --prey RUN_ID|scripted --seed N [--speed V]` calls `replay.run_match`.
  - `train` merges the user's overrides into `registry.default_params` before `train.train_run` (AD-10), and prints one progress line per curve point, with every learning side's score.
  - Returns 0 on success.
- **Errors:** 2 with the valid choices on `UnknownName`; 1 naming the missing item on a missing run or file
- **Used by:** the `rllab` console script

size: int; names: tuple[str, ...]; max_norm: float | None; score_names: tuple[str, ...]`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - `discrete`: `size` moves named by `names`; a move is an `int` in `[0, size)`; `max_norm` is `None`.\n  - `continuous`: a move is a float32 vector of length `size`, its components named by `names`; the environment caps its length at `max_norm`.\n  - `score_names` names the numbers `Agent.scores` gives per agent: `straight, left, right` in Snake, `spread` for any fish (AD-11).\n- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, registry, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 moves; n_agents: int; obs_size: int; actions: ActionSpace`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - An environment's sides keep a fixed order, and their names are unique within it (AD-16).\n  - Snake has one side: `snake`, 1 agent, 11 obs, discrete.\n  - The tank has one side: `fish`, 1 or 5 agents, 11 obs, capped at 2.5.\n  - Predator-prey has two sides, in this order: `predator` (1 agent, 11 obs, capped at 3.0), then `prey` (5 agents, 14 obs, capped at 2.5).\n- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, registry, evaluate, train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Side/ · **Cases:** Snake's side; a 5-fish tank side; predator-prey's two sides\n- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, registry, evaluate, train, replay\n\n### core.Step: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; rewards: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; acting: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; ended: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; done: bool; score: tuple[float, ...]; ending: str | None`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - Every tuple has one entry per side, in `Env.sides` order (AD-16).\n  - `obs[s]` is float32 `(n_agents, obs_size)` of side `s`; rows of agents not acting are all 0.\n  - `rewards[s]` is float `(n_agents,)`, 0 for agents that didn't act.\n  - `acting[s]` is bool: who moves next.\n  - `ended[s]` is bool: whose experience ended on this step (removed, caught, killed, or the game ended).\n  - `done` holds exactly when no agent of any side acts.\n  - `score[s]` is side `s`'s game score so far: Snake's food eaten; the tank's TANK-10 mean seconds survived; predator-prey's `(catches, pellets per prey)` (HUNT-11).\n  - `ending` is `None` until `done`, then a name the environment defines: Snake's `wall`, `body`, `starved` or `full_board`; the tank's `all_removed` or `time_cap`; predator-prey's `all_caught` or `time_cap`.\n- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, evaluate, train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** an ordinary Snake move; a food move; a wall death; a tank step removing 1 of 5 fish; a tank step reaching the cap; a predator-prey step with a catch and a pellet eaten\n- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, evaluate, train, replay\n\n### core.Transition: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: agent: int; obs: np.ndarray; action: int | np.ndarray; reward: float; next_obs: np.ndarray; done: bool`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - One agent's step; `agent` counts within its side.\n  - `obs` and `next_obs` are float32 `(obs_size,)` of its side.\n  - `action` is an `int` for discrete moves, or the float32 vector the agent chose (before any cap) for continuous ones.\n  - `done` is that agent's `Step.ended`; when `done`, every learner ignores `next_obs`.\n- **Boundary:** train, tabular, dqn, pg · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a mid-game Snake move; a game-ending Snake move; a tank fish's move; a tank fish's removal; a predator's catch; a prey's capture\n- **Used by:** train, tabular, dqn, pg\n\n### core.Shape: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Shape: kind: Literal[\"cell\", \"circle\", \"ring\"]; x: float; y: float; r: float; style: str; agent: tuple[int, int] | None`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - Positions are in world units. A `cell` is the unit square with corner `(x, y)`, and `r` is unused. A `circle` is filled and a `ring` outlined, centred at `(x, y)` with radius `r`.\n  - `style` names a colour: `head`, `body`, `food`, `tank`, `ball`, `fish`, `predator`, `prey` or `pellet`. An unknown style gets a fallback colour.\n  - `agent` is set, as a `(side, agent)` pair, on the shape an agent's arrow starts from: Snake's head, each fish (AD-16).\n- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Shape/ · **Cases:** a Snake head cell; a fish circle with its agent; the tank ring; a prey circle with its agent\n- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, replay\n\n### core.Frame: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: bounds: tuple[float, float, float, float]; y_down: bool; shapes: tuple[Shape, ...]; labels: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - `bounds` is `(x_min, y_min, x_max, y_max)`, and every shape lies inside it.\n  - `y_down` holds when y grows downward. Snake: `(0, 0, 10, 10)`, true. The tank and predator-prey: `(-10, -10, 10, 10)`, false.\n  - Shapes draw in order.\n  - `labels` are name/value lines (REPLAY-02, REPLAY-08, REPLAY-10). Snake: food eaten. The tank: time in seconds, fish swimming, and each removed fish's removal time. Predator-prey: time in seconds, pellets eaten, prey caught, and each caught prey's capture time.\n- **Boundary:** snake, tank, hunt, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** a Snake start frame; a Snake frame at length 5; a 5-fish tank frame after one removal; a predator-prey frame after one catch; a test-env frame\n- **Used by:** snake, tank, hunt, replay\n\n### core.Env: type\n`class Env(Protocol): name: str; sides: tuple[Side, ...]; def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, ...]; def step(self, moves: tuple[np.ndarray, ...]) -> Step; def frame(self) -> Frame`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - `reset` returns float32 `(n_agents, obs_size)` per side, with every agent acting.\n  - `moves[s]` is int64 `(n_agents,)` for discrete moves, or float32 `(n_agents, size)` for continuous ones. Rows of agents not acting are ignored.\n  - The same seed and moves give identical `Step`s and `Frame`s (AD-11, AD-16).\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a discrete move outside `[0, size)`, a non-finite continuous move, or the wrong number of sides or shape; `RuntimeError` for `step` before `reset` or after `done`\n- **Boundary:** registry, evaluate, train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of three recorded games from seed 7: a 20-move Snake game, a 30-step 5-fish tank game, and a 30-step predator-prey game\n- **Used by:** registry, evaluate, train, tournament, replay; PL-01, PL-03\n\n### core.Agent: type\n`class Agent(Protocol): name: str; def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray; def scores(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray | None`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - An agent plays one side: `obs` is that side's rows, and it returns one move per row, shaped as that side's `Env.step` entry. Each row is decided on its own (PG-06).\n  - With `explore=False`, a value learner picks its highest score, ties by move order (straight, left, right), and a policy learner picks its most likely move.\n  - `scores` is `(rows, len(score_names))`, or `None` for baselines.\n- **Boundary:** registry, evaluate, train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of the scripted bot over a recorded 20-move game, a table learner with scores, a PPO policy over a recorded 5-fish game, and the scripted chaser over a recorded predator-prey game\n- **Used by:** registry, colearn, evaluate, train, replay; PL-01, PL-03\n\n### core.Learner: type\n`class Learner(Agent, Protocol): updates: int; def observe(self, t: Transition, step: int, rng: np.random.Generator) -> None; def frozen(self) -> Agent; def settings(self) -> dict[str, int | float | str]; def save(self, path: Path) -> None; @classmethod def load(cls, path: Path) -> Learner`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - `observe` is the only place learning happens. It is called once per acting agent of the learner's side per step, in agent order.\n  - `updates` counts the learning updates applied so far, starting at 0. For the table learner and the plain DQN rung, each `observe` is one update.\n  - `act(explore=True)` is the training behaviour. Value learners explore at `Explore.rate(step)`; policy learners sample from the policy.\n  - `frozen()` returns a copy that acts exactly as the learner does now and never changes (AD-17).\n  - A loaded learner acts exactly like the saved one.\n- **Errors:** `load` raises `FileNotFoundError` or `ValueError` for a missing or unreadable file\n- **Boundary:** registry, colearn, train, store · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Learner/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of a table learner over 50 recorded transitions, a plain DQN rung, and a REINFORCE rung over a recorded 1-fish game; a PPO rung's frozen copy after 2 updates\n- **Used by:** registry, colearn, train, store; PL-01\n\n### core.Lineup: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Lineup: agents: tuple[Agent, ...]; learns: tuple[bool, ...]`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - One entry per side, in `Env.sides` order.\n  - `agents[s]` plays side `s`. Where `learns[s]` is true, `agents[s]` is that side's `Learner`, and only those sides' learners observe (AD-17).\n- **Boundary:** colearn, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Lineup/ · **Cases:** Snake's learner alone; rung 2 with the predator learning against the prey's own frozen learner; rung 3 with the prey learning against a pool copy; the prey control against the scripted chaser\n- **Used by:** colearn, train; PL-01\n\n### core.Schedule: type\n`class Schedule(Protocol): learners: tuple[Learner | None, ...]; versions: int; def start_game(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Lineup; def current(self) -> Lineup`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - `learners[s]` is side `s`'s learner, or `None` for a side the run doesn't train. The sides with a learner are the run's learning sides.\n  - `versions` is 10 for a predator-prey ladder rung and 0 otherwise (TOUR-01).\n  - `start_game` is called at each training game's start; it is where opponents are drawn, with `rng` (COEV-04).\n  - `current` is called after every step and reflects any switch due after that step's updates (COEV-03).\n  - The same calls and the same learner updates give the same lineups.\n- **Boundary:** registry, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Schedule/ · **Cases:** replay doubles of `solo` over 3 games, of `turns` across its first swap, and of `pool` over 12 recorded draws\n- **Used by:** registry, train; PL-01\n\n### core.Explore: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Explore: start: float; floor: float; decay_steps: int` with `def rate(self, step: int) -> float`\n- **Invariants:** falls linearly from `start` at step 0 to `floor` at `decay_steps`, then stays; `1 ≥ start ≥ floor ≥ 0` (TAB-02, DQN-06)\n- **Boundary:** tabular, dqn · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Explore/ · **Cases:** 1.0 → 0.05 over 100,000 steps, read at 0, 50,000 and 200,000\n- **Used by:** tabular, dqn\n\n## snake\n\n### snake.SnakeEnv: The Snake game\n`class SnakeEnv(Env): def __init__(self, reward: Literal[\"plain\", \"shaped\"] = \"plain\") -> None`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - `name=\"snake\"`, with one side, `snake`: 1 agent, discrete moves `straight, left, right`, and 11 obs in `Fact` order.\n  - A fixed 10×10 board, drawn as cells.\n  - `score` counts food whatever the reward (SHAPE-04).\n  - Rewards per SNAKE-03–SNAKE-05, SNAKE-07, SHAPE-01 and SHAPE-02.\n- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; the obs with the head at the top wall heading up\n- **Used by:** baselines\n\n## tank\n\n### tank.TankEnv: The fish tank\n`class TankEnv(Env): def __init__(self, fish: Literal[1, 5] = 1, view: Literal[\"short\", \"wide\"] = \"short\") -> None`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - `name=\"tank\"`, with one side, `fish`: `fish` agents, continuous moves `vx, vy` capped at 2.5, `score_names=(\"spread\",)`, and 11 obs in `View` order.\n  - Tank radius 10. Ball radius 0.5, moving at 2 units per second. Fish radius 0.25.\n  - A step is 0.1 s, and the cap is 600 steps.\n  - View radius 4 (`short`) or 20 (`wide`).\n  - Step order per AD-13; rewards per TANK-09; `score` per TANK-10 (TANK-01–TANK-13).\n- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `ValueError` for an unknown `fish` or `view`\n- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tank.TankEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 1-fish game from seed 7; one of a 5-fish wide-view game with a removal and a bounce\n- **Used by:** registry; PL-01\n\n### tank.View: type\n`class View(IntEnum): BALL_SEEN=0; BALL_X=1; BALL_Y=2; BALL_VX=3; BALL_VY=4; WALL_SEEN=5; WALL_X=6; WALL_Y=7; FISH_SEEN=8; FISH_X=9; FISH_Y=10`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - Each number's index in an obs row.\n  - Positions, offsets and velocities are relative to the fish, in tank directions.\n  - `*_SEEN` is 0.0 or 1.0, and an unseen item's numbers are 0 (TANK-11).\n  - When two other fish are equally near, the one with the lower fish number counts as nearest.\n- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tank.View/ · **Cases:** the short view with the ball 3 units away; the short view at the tank's centre; a 1-fish view\n- **Used by:** baselines\n\n## hunt\n\n### hunt.HuntEnv: Predator and prey\n`class HuntEnv(Env): def __init__(self) -> None`\n- **Invariants:**\n  - `name=\"hunt\"`, with two sides, in this order: `predator` (1 agent, 11 obs in `PredatorView` order, capped at 3.0) and `prey` (5 agents, 14 obs in `PreyView` order, capped at 2.5).
