predator unseen, pellet at (−3, 0); nothing seen\n- **Used by:** registry; PL-01\n\n## tabular\n\n### tabular.TabularSettings: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TabularSettings: lr: float; gamma: float; explore: Explore`\n- **Invariants:** `0 < lr ≤ 1`; `0 ≤ gamma < 1`\n- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tabular.TabularSettings/ · **Cases:** lr 0.1, gamma 0.9, explore 1.0 → 0.01 over 50,000\n- **Used by:** registry\n\n### tabular.TabularQ: Table-based Q-learner\n`class TabularQ(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: TabularSettings, n_obs: int = 11, n_actions: int = 3) -> None`\n- **Invariants:** discrete moves only; one row of scores per distinct obs, starting at 0; `observe` applies `Q ← Q + lr·(r + gamma·max Q(next) − Q)`, with no next term when `done` (TAB-01), and counts one update; saves `.npz`\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the obs length isn't `n_obs`\n- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tabular.TabularQ/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed update; a game-ending update\n- **Used by:** registry; PL-01\n\n## dqn\n\n### dqn.DQNSettings: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class DQNSettings: rung: Literal[\"plain\", \"replay\", \"target\", \"double\"]; lr: float; gamma: float; hidden: tuple[int, ...]; explore: Explore; batch_size: int; buffer_size: int; target_every: int`\n- **Invariants:** each rung adds one piece to the one before (AD-03); `plain` ignores `batch_size`, `buffer_size`, `target_every`; `hidden` defaults to `(64, 64)`\n- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/dqn.DQNSettings/ · **Cases:** one per rung, all other fields equal\n- **Used by:** registry\n\n### dqn.DQNAgent: DQN learner\n`class DQNAgent(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: DQNSettings, n_obs: int, n_actions: int, torch_seed: int) -> None`\n- **Invariants:** discrete moves only; CPU only; target `r + gamma·Q′(next, a*)`: `Q′` is the learner for `plain`/`replay`, the frozen copy for `target`/`double`; `a*` is `Q′`'s argmax, except the learner's under `double`; no next term when `done`; batches wait for a full store; the copy refreshes every `target_every` updates (DQN-01–DQN-04); `updates` counts gradient steps; saves `.pt`\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `buffer_size < batch_size` from `replay` up\n- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/dqn.DQNAgent/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed target per rung\n- **Used by:** registry; PL-01\n\n## pg\n\n### pg.PGSettings: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PGSettings: rung: Literal[\"reinforce\", \"baseline\", \"actor_critic\", \"ppo\"]; lr: float; critic_lr: float; gamma: float; hidden: tuple[int, ...]; init_log_spread: float; steps_per_update: int; passes: int; minibatch: int; clip: float`\n- **Invariants:** each rung adds one piece to the one before (AD-12); `reinforce` ignores `critic_lr`, `steps_per_update`, `passes`, `minibatch`, `clip`; `baseline` adds `critic_lr`; `actor_critic` adds `steps_per_update`; `ppo` adds `passes`, `minibatch`, `clip`; `hidden` defaults to `(64, 64)`; `0 ≤ gamma < 1`, `0 < clip < 1`\n- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGSettings/ · **Cases:** one per rung, all other fields equal\n- **Used by:** registry\n\n### pg.PGAgent: Policy-gradient learner\n`class PGAgent(Learner): def __init__(self, settings: PGSettings, n_obs: int, action_size: int, torch_seed: int) -> None`\n- **Invariants:** continuous moves only; CPU only; a diagonal Gaussian policy per obs row, log spread clamped to [−5, 1]; `act(explore=True)` samples with `rng`, `explore=False` returns the mean (PG-07); `scores` is the mean of the two spreads; learns from the sampled move; `reinforce`/`baseline` update once every agent's experience has ended, `actor_critic`/`ppo` every `steps_per_update` steps; weights per PG-01–PG-04, no next term when `done`\n- **Invariants (sides):** every fish of its side trains the one policy (PG-06), and each predator-prey side is its own `PGAgent` (COEV-01); every update goes through the `pg` functions below, `last_update: UpdateLog | None` holds the latest one, `None` before the first (AD-15); `updates` counts them; `frozen()` copies the policy alone; saves `.pt`\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `minibatch > steps_per_update` under `ppo`\n- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGAgent/ · **Cases:** one hand-computed update per rung; a PPO move already past the clip; a 14-obs prey update over a 5-prey batch with a capture\n- **Used by:** registry; PL-01\n\n### pg.UpdateLog: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class UpdateLog: rung: str; weights: torch.Tensor; log_probs: torch.Tensor; values: torch.Tensor | None; critic_loss: tuple[float, float] | None; minibatches: tuple[torch.Tensor, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** one entry per move the update learned from, in fish order then time order; `weights` from `move_weights`; `log_probs` from `gaussian_log_prob` at the sampled move, before training changes the policy; `values` and `critic_loss` (squared error to the targets before and after the critic's step) are `None` under `reinforce`; `minibatches` is `minibatch_plan`'s output under `ppo`, and one index tensor covering every move otherwise (AD-15)\n- **Boundary:** tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.UpdateLog/ · **Cases:** a REINFORCE update over a 12-step 1-fish game; a PPO update over a 5-fish batch\n- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29\n\n### pg.discounted_returns: Discounted returns\n`def discounted_returns(rewards: torch.Tensor, gamma: float) -> torch.Tensor`\n- **Invariants:** `rewards` float32 `(T,)` is one fish's moves in time order, its experience ending at the last move; returns `G_t = Σ_k gamma^k r_{t+k}`, same shape and dtype (PG-01)\n- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29\n\n### pg.move_weights: Each rung's weight per move\n`def move_weights(rung: str, rewards: torch.Tensor, done: torch.Tensor, values: torch.Tensor | None, next_values: torch.Tensor | None, gamma: float) -> torch.Tensor`\n- **Invariants:** inputs `(T,)`, one fish's moves in time order; `reinforce`: `G_t`; `baseline`: `G_t − V(s_t)`; `actor_critic`, `ppo`: `r_t + gamma·V(s_{t+1}) − V(s_t)`, no next term where `done` (PG-01–PG-04); never part of the gradient\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` for an unknown rung, or `values`/`next_values` missing where the rung needs them\n- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29\n\n### pg.gaussian_log_prob: Log-probability of a swim\n`def gaussian_log_prob(mean: torch.Tensor, log_spread: torch.Tensor, action: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor`\n- **Invariants:** inputs `(N, size)`, `log_spread` already clamped to [−5, 1]; returns `(N,)` `Σ_i [−½((a_i − μ_i)/σ_i)² − log σ_i − ½ log 2π]`, differentiable in `mean` and `log_spread`\n- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29\n\n### pg.ppo_objective: Clipped objective per move\n`def ppo_objective(log_prob: torch.Tensor, old_log_prob: torch.Tensor, weight: torch.Tensor, clip: float) -> torch.Tensor`\n- **Invariants:** inputs `(N,)`; `ρ = exp(log_prob − old_log_prob)`; returns `(N,)` `min(ρ·A, clip(ρ, 1 − clip, 1 + clip)·A)`; a move with `ρ > 1 + clip` and `A > 0`, or `ρ < 1 − clip` and `A < 0`, has gradient exactly 0 (PG-04)\n- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29\n\n### pg.minibatch_plan: Passes over a batch\n`def minibatch_plan(n_moves: int, passes: int, minibatch: int, gen: torch.Generator) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** `passes` passes in order, each a fresh permutation of `range(n_moves)` from `gen` cut into minibatches of `minibatch` (the last may be shorter); every move appears once per pass (PG-04)\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` if any argument is below 1\n- **Used by:** pg; PL-01; TC-29\n\n## colearn\n\n### colearn.Pool: type\n`class Pool: def __init__(self, start: Agent, size: int = 10) -> None; copies: tuple[Agent, ...]; def add(self, agent: Agent) -> None; def draw(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Agent`\n- **Invariants:** starts holding `start` alone; the first `add` replaces it, and from then on it keeps the latest `size` copies, oldest first; `draw` picks uniformly from `copies`, the latest included (COEV-04 and its edge case)\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `size` is below 1\n- **Boundary:** tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/colearn.Pool/ · **Cases:** a fresh pool; 11 copies added; 10,000 draws from 10 copies with rng seed 3\n- **Used by:** colearn\n\n### colearn.make_schedule: Build a schedule\n`def make_schedule(kind: Literal[\"solo\", \"together\", \"turns\", \"pool\", \"control\"], learners: tuple[Learner | None, ...], bots: tuple[Agent | None, ...], *, swap_every: int = 20, copy_every: int = 20, pool_size: int = 10) -> Schedule`\n- **Invariants:** one entry per side in `learners` and `bots`, each side having exactly one; `solo`: one side, always learning; `together`: both learn every step (COEV-02); `turns`: side 0 first, a side stops learning straight after its `swap_every`-th update of the turn, and the other starts at the next `start_game` (COEV-03); `pool`: as `turns`, the side not learning played by a draw from its `Pool` at each `start_game`, each side adding `frozen()` every `copy_every` of its updates (COEV-04); `control`: the learner's side always learns, against the other side's bot (COEV-05); `versions` 10 for `together`, `turns`, `pool`, else 0 (AD-17)\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a kind that doesn't fit the given sides, or an interval below 1\n- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/colearn.make_schedule/ · **Cases:** `solo` for Snake; `together`; `turns` over 45 recorded predator updates; `pool` over 12 games; the predator `control`\n- **Used by:** registry; PL-01\n\n## registry\n\n### registry.UnknownName: type\n`class UnknownName(ValueError): kind: str; name: str; choices: tuple[str, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** `kind` is `env`, `setup`, `match`, `side`, `learner` or `run`; `choices` lists the valid names for that environment; the message lists them (RUN-01 edge case)\n- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.UnknownName/ · **Cases:** an unknown reward; a Snake learner asked for in the tank; an unknown predator-prey match\n- **Used by:** cli, train\n\n### registry.make_env: Build an environment\n`def make_env(env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str]) -> Env`\n- **Invariants:** a fresh environment per call; `env` in `ENVS` (`snake`, `tank`, `hunt`; tests register a second); `setup` is `{\"reward\": \"plain\" | \"shaped\" | \"none\"}` for Snake (`none` plays plain, for baselines), `{\"fish\": 1 | 5, \"view\": \"short\" | \"wide\"}` for the tank, and for predator-prey `{\"match\": \"rung-1\" | \"rung-2\" | \"rung-3\" | \"control-predator\" | \"control-prey\"}` (learners) or `{\"side\": \"predator\" | \"prey\"}` (baselines), all building the same `HuntEnv`\n- **Errors:** `UnknownName` for an unknown environment, setup key or setup value\n- **Boundary:** train, tournament, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_env/ · **Cases:** `snake`/`shaped`; `tank`/5 fish/`wide`; `hunt`/`rung-3`; an unknown reward; `reward` given to the tank\n- **Used by:** train, tournament, replay; PL-01, PL-03\n\n### registry.default_params: A run's full settings\n`def default_params(learner: str, *, env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str]) -> dict[str, int | float | str]`\n- **Invariants:** every setting the run uses, flattened as `RunSettings.params` (`lr`, `explore.start`…); `{}` for `random` and `scripted`; predator-prey's `pg-ppo` prefixes each trained side's settings with its name (`predator.lr`, `prey.clip`…) and adds `colearn.swap_every` from rung 2 and `colearn.copy_every`, `colearn.pool_size` on rung 3 (AD-17); the four `dqn-*` names differ only in `rung`, the four `pg-*` names likewise, and predator-prey's setups only by those added settings and which sides have settings (AD-03, AD-10, AD-12)\n- **Errors:** `UnknownName`\n- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.default_params/ · **Cases:** `tabular`; `dqn-double`; `pg-ppo` on the tank; `pg-ppo` on `rung-3`; `pg-ppo` on `control-prey`; `scripted`\n- **Used by:** cli\n\n### registry.make_agents: Build a run's agents from settings\n`def make_agents(learner: str, params: Mapping[str, int | float | str], *, env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str], init_seeds: tuple[int, ...]) -> tuple[Agent | None, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** one entry per side: the agent for each side the run trains or records, `None` for a side played only by its opponents; Snake's learners are `tabular`, `dqn-plain`, `dqn-replay`, `dqn-target`, `dqn-double`; the tank's are `pg-reinforce`, `pg-baseline`, `pg-actor-critic`, `pg-ppo`; predator-prey's is `pg-ppo`, on both sides for a rung and the named side for a control (COEV-01, COEV-05); `random` and `scripted` build the environment's baselines, on `setup[\"side\"]` in predator-prey; `params` holds every `default_params` key (AD-10); learners are `Learner`s seeded from their side's `init_seeds` entry\n- **Errors:** `UnknownName`, its choices the environment's own names, when `learner` is unknown or belongs to another environment; `ValueError` for invalid or missing params\n- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_agents/ · **Cases:** `tabular` with defaults; `pg-ppo` on the tank with defaults; `pg-ppo` on `rung-1`; `pg-ppo` on `control-predator`; `scripted` on side `prey`; `dqn-triple`; `dqn-plain` for the tank\n- **Used by:** train; PL-01\n\n### registry.make_schedule: Build a run's schedule\n`def make_schedule(env: str, setup: Mapping[str, int | str], agents: tuple[Agent | None, ...], params: Mapping[str, int | float | str]) -> Schedule`\n- **Invariants:** `solo` for Snake and the tank; predator-prey's `rung-1`, `rung-2`, `rung-3` get `together`, `turns`, `pool`, and `control-predator`, `control-prey` get `control` with the other side's scripted bot; intervals from `params` (AD-17); never called for a baseline record, which `train` only tests\n- **Errors:** `UnknownName` for an unknown match\n- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_schedule/ · **Cases:** Snake's `solo`; `rung-2`; `control-prey`\n- **Used by:** train; PL-01\n\n### registry.test_lineup: Each side's fixed test opponents\n`def test_lineup(env: str, side: int, agent: Agent) -> tuple[Agent, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** `agent` plays side `side` and every other side plays its scripted bot: the scripted chaser for the predator, the scripted forager for the prey (EVAL-01, BASE-07); `(agent,)` in a one-side environment\n- **Errors:** `IndexError` for a side the environment doesn't have\n- **Boundary:** train, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.test_lineup/ · **Cases:** Snake's learner; a learning predator; the random prey\n- **Used by:** train, replay; PL-01, PL-03\n\n### registry.load_agent: Load a saved agent\n`def load_agent(record: RunRecord, *, side: str | None = None, version: int | None = None) -> Agent`\n- **Invariants:** `side` defaults to the run's only trained or recorded side; a baseline record rebuilds that side's baseline; a learner record loads `agent-<side>`, or `versions/<side>-<version>` when `version` is given (TOUR-01)\n- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the missing file; `ValueError` if `side` is needed and missing, or `version` is given for a run with no versions\n- **Boundary:** replay, tournament · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.load_agent/ · **Cases:** a saved table learner; a scripted-bot record; a saved PPO policy; a rung-2 run's prey version 4\n- **Used by:** replay, tournament; PL-03\n\n## evaluate\n\n### evaluate.TestGame: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TestGame: seed: int; side: int; moves: tuple[tuple[np.ndarray, ...], ...]; scores: tuple[tuple[np.ndarray | None, ...], ...]; score_after: tuple[tuple[float, ...], ...]; ending: str; score: tuple[float, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** `side` is the tested side; one `moves`, `scores` and `score_after` entry per step, each with one item per side (AD-16); a `moves` item is that side's `Env.step` input, rows of agents not acting 0; a `scores` item is `(n_agents, len(score_names))`, rows of agents not acting NaN, or `None` for a side played by a baseline; `score == score_after[-1]`\n- **Boundary:** train, tournament, store, replay, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.TestGame/ · **Cases:** a table-learner game ending on a wall; a scripted-bot game ending starved; a one-move game; a 5-fish PPO game reaching the cap; a learning predator's game against the scripted forager ending `all_caught`\n- **Used by:** train, tournament, store, replay, compare\n\n### evaluate.TestResult: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TestResult: side: int; games: tuple[TestGame, ...]; score: float`\n- **Invariants:** one game per seed played, in seed order; `score` is the mean of the games' `score[side]` (EVAL-02)\n- **Boundary:** train, tournament · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.TestResult/ · **Cases:** 100 recorded scripted-bot games; 100 recorded flee-bot games; 100 recorded prey games against the scripted chaser\n- **Used by:** train, tournament\n\n### evaluate.play_game: Play one greedy game\n`def play_game(env: Env, lineup: tuple[Agent, ...], seed: int, rng: np.random.Generator, *, side: int = 0) -> TestGame`\n- **Invariants:** `act(explore=False)` on every side's acting rows at every step; `rng` reaches only agents that ignore `explore`; same inputs → same game\n- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.play_game/ · **Cases:** the scripted bot on seed 7; the flee bot with 5 fish on seed 7; the scripted chaser against the scripted forager on seed 7\n- **Used by:** replay, train, tournament; PL-01, PL-03\n\n### evaluate.test_agent: Score a side on the test games\n`def test_agent(env: Env, lineup: tuple[Agent, ...], *, side: int = 0, seeds: Sequence[int] = range(100), rng_seed: int = 0) -> TestResult`\n- **Invariants:** plays `seeds` of the given environment setup, each a test seed in 0–99, never used in training (EVAL-01); scores side `side` by `Step.score`, never the training reward (SHAPE-04)\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a seed outside 0–99\n- **Boundary:** train, tournament · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.test_agent/ · **Cases:** the scripted bot's 100 recorded games; the flee bot's 100 recorded 1-fish games; the random predator's 100 recorded games against the scripted forager\n- **Used by:** train, tournament; PL-01\n\n## store\n\n### store.RunSettings: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunSettings: env: str; learner: str; setup: Mapping[str, int | str]; seed: int; train_steps: int; params: Mapping[str, int | float | str]; curve_points: int = 20; max_minutes: float = 60.0`\n- **Invariants:** `setup` as `registry.make_env` takes it, so Snake's names its reward (SHAPE-03), the tank's its fish count and view, and predator-prey's its match or a baseline's side; `params` holds every setting of the run, flattened (`explore.start`, `predator.lr`…): `default_params` with the user's overrides (AD-10); `train_steps` counts environment steps; saved with library versions, enough to recreate the run (RUN-03); a baseline has `train_steps=0`, Snake's with reward `none`\n- **Boundary:** cli, train, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.RunSettings/ · **Cases:** a tabular plain run; a double-DQN shaped run; a scripted-bot record; a 5-fish wide-view PPO run; a 1-fish flee-bot record; a rung-3 run; a random-prey record\n- **Used by:** cli, train, compare\n\n### store.CurvePoint: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePoint: steps: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; elapsed_s: float`\n- **Invariants:** `steps` strictly increasing within a run; `scores` holds one `TestResult.score` per learning side, keyed by side name (RUN-04, EVAL-03)\n- **Boundary:** train, compare, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.CurvePoint/ · **Cases:** the 20 points of a recorded tabular run; 3 points of a rung-1 run\n- **Used by:** train, compare, cli\n\n### store.RunRecord: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: run_id: str; path: Path; settings: RunSettings; status: Literal[\"complete\", \"incomplete\", \"over_time\"]; curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]; missing: tuple[str, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** `complete` means all its evidence is present: settings, curve, each trained side's agent, any versions, test games (RUN-02, TOUR-01); `missing` names absent files; a folder left `running` reads as `incomplete`\n- **Boundary:** train, tournament, compare, replay, registry, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a complete run; an over-time run; a run missing its test games after a disk error; a complete tank run; a complete rung-2 run; a rung-3 run missing prey version 7\n- **Used by:** train, tournament, compare, replay, registry, cli\n\n### store.StagedRun: type\n`class StagedRun(Protocol): run_id: str; path: Path; def add_point(self, point: CurvePoint) -> None; def add_version(self, side: str, k: int, learner: Learner) -> None; def finish(self, status: Literal[\"complete\", \"incomplete\", \"over_time\"], *, agents: Mapping[str, Agent], games: Sequence[TestGame]) -> RunRecord`\n- **Invariants:** `add_point` writes at once; `add_version` saves `versions/<side>-<k>.pt`, `k` 1–10 (TOUR-01); `finish` writes each trained side's agent and the games (moves as exact JSON numbers, AD-06), then `status.json` last; every file written by rename; a failed write gives `incomplete` with `missing` set\n- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.StagedRun/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a 3-point run finished complete; one finished incomplete after an `OSError`; one of a rung-1 run with 2 versions per side\n- **Used by:** train; PL-01\n\n### store.begin_run: Start a run folder\n`def begin_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path(\"runs\")) -> StagedRun`\n- **Invariants:** a new folder `<env>/<learner>/<setup>/seed-<n>/<UTC timestamp>-<id>/` every time, never reused (RUN-05); `<setup>` is the reward in Snake, `fish-<n>-<view>` in the tank, and the match or the baseline's side in predator-prey; writes `settings.json` and a `running` status\n- **Errors:** `OSError` if the folder can't be created\n- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.begin_run/ · **Cases:** two runs with identical settings; a tank run's folder name; a predator-prey control's folder name\n- **Used by:** train; PL-01\n\n### store.list_runs: Find runs\n`def list_runs(*, root: Path = Path(\"runs\"), env: str | None = None, learner: str | None = None, setup: str | None = None, complete_only: bool = False) -> list[RunRecord]`\n- **Invariants:** `setup` is the folder label; sorted by environment, learner, setup, seed, run id; an unreadable folder comes back `incomplete` with `missing` set, never skipped\n- **Boundary:** tournament, compare, replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.list_runs/ · **Cases:** a runs folder with one complete, one over-time and one broken run; one with Snake, tank and predator-prey runs\n- **Used by:** tournament, compare, replay, cli; PL-02, PL-03\n\n### store.load_test_games: Read a run's test games\n`def load_test_games(record: RunRecord) -> list[TestGame]`\n- **Invariants:** each tested side's games in side order, then seeds 0–99 in order, exactly as saved, moves bit for bit (AD-06)\n- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run and file\n- **Boundary:** replay, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.load_test_games/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake run's 100 games; a complete 5-fish run's 100 games; a complete rung-1 run's 200 games\n- **Used by:** replay, compare; PL-02, PL-03\n\n### store.TournamentGrid: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TournamentGrid: run_id: str; setup: str; seed: int; seeds: tuple[int, ...]; catches: np.ndarray`\n- **Invariants:** `catches` float `(10, 10)`, row `i` predator version `i + 1`, column `j` prey version `j + 1`, each cell the mean predator score (HUNT-11) over `seeds`; `seeds` are test seeds 0–19 (TOUR-02, AD-19)\n- **Boundary:** tournament, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.TournamentGrid/ · **Cases:** a rung-1 grid that cycles; a rung-3 grid rising with the predator's version\n- **Used by:** tournament, compare; PL-02\n\n### store.save_tournament: Save a run's tournament\n`def save_tournament(record: RunRecord, grid: TournamentGrid, *, root: Path = Path(\"tournaments\")) -> Path`\n- **Invariants:** writes `<env>/<learner>/<setup>/seed-<n>/<run id>.json` by rename, once (AD-19)\n- **Errors:** `FileExistsError` if the run already has a grid; `OSError` on a failed write\n- **Boundary:** tournament · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.save_tournament/ · **Cases:** a first save; a second save of the same run\n- **Used by:** tournament\n\n### store.load_tournament: Read a run's tournament\n`def load_tournament(record: RunRecord, *, root: Path = Path(\"tournaments\")) -> TournamentGrid | None`\n- **Invariants:** `None` when the run has no grid; otherwise the grid exactly as saved\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the file if it is unreadable\n- **Boundary:** tournament, compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/store.load_tournament/ · **Cases:** a saved grid; a run with none\n- **Used by:** tournament, compare; PL-02\n\n## train\n\n### train.train_run: One training run\n`def train_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path(\"runs\"), progress: Callable[[CurvePoint], None] | None = None) -> RunRecord`\n- **Invariants:** one environment setup, one seed, the agents of `registry.make_agents` and the schedule of `registry.make_schedule` (RUN-01, AD-17); each training game starts with `start_game`; each step, every side's lineup agent acts on its acting rows with `explore=True`, each learning side observes once per acting agent in agent order (PG-06, AD-11), then `current`; `curve_points` evenly spaced test rounds, each learning side against `registry.test_lineup`, each point passed to `progress` (RUN-04)\n- **Invariants (run):** with `versions` 10, each learning side is saved at every second point (TOUR-01); a baseline plays its test lineup once; stops at `max_minutes` as `over_time` (RUN-07); Ctrl-C ends `incomplete` (RUN-06); same settings → same curve (RUN-03)\n- **Errors:** `UnknownName` before any folder exists; a save failure returns an `incomplete` record\n- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train_run/ · **Cases:** a recorded 3-point tabular run; an over-time run; a recorded 3-point 5-fish PPO run; a recorded 4-point rung-2 run with 2 versions per side\n- **Used by:** cli; PL-01\n\n## tournament\n\n### tournament.play_tournament: Play one run's tournament\n`def play_tournament(record: RunRecord, *, seeds: Sequence[int] = range(20)) -> TournamentGrid`\n- **Invariants:** `record` is a complete predator-prey ladder run; each of its 10 predator versions plays each of its 10 prey versions, both with `explore=False`, on `seeds`, each cell the mean predator score (TOUR-02); same record and seeds → same grid\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` for a run that isn't a complete ladder run; `FileNotFoundError` naming a missing version\n- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tournament.play_tournament/ · **Cases:** a recorded rung-1 run whose versions are random and scripted stand-ins; a control run\n- **Used by:** tournament\n\n### tournament.run_tournaments: Play every missing tournament\n`def run_tournaments(*, runs: Path = Path(\"runs\"), out: Path = Path(\"tournaments\"), progress: Callable[[str], None] | None = None) -> tuple[TournamentGrid, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** plays and saves a grid for every complete predator-prey ladder run without one, in `list_runs` order, passing each run id to `progress`; returns the grids it made; never replays a saved grid (AD-19)\n- **Errors:** a run that fails is reported through `progress` and left without a grid, never raised\n- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/tournament.run_tournaments/ · **Cases:** a fake over two ladder runs, one already played\n- **Used by:** cli\n\n## compare\n\n### compare.Comparison: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: comparison_id: str; env: str; kind: Literal[\"baseline\", \"ladder\", \"tournament\"]; side: str | None; a: str; b: str; outcome: Literal[\"beats\", \"ties\", \"loses\", \"better\", \"worse\", \"no_difference\", \"trend\", \"no_trend\"]; test: str; p_adjusted: float; note: str | None; explanation: str | None`\n- **Invariants:** `a` and `b` name a learner and setup, or a baseline and setup (a tournament names its rung in both); `side` names the compared side in a multi-side environment; a tournament's `p_adjusted` is the larger of its two trend tests' (AD-19); `note` is set on a predator-prey control step: the control trained against that scripted bot, its test opponent (EVAL-08); explained exactly when `explanation` is non-empty (EVAL-06); `comparison_id` names the explanation file under `reports/<env>/explanations/`\n- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Comparison/ · **Cases:** an explained tie; an unexplained no-difference; a beat; a tank ladder step; a predator control step with its note; an unexplained no-trend tournament\n- **Used by:** cli\n\n### compare.Report: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Report: env: str; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; waiting: tuple[str, ...]; done: bool; not_done: tuple[str, ...]; path: Path`\n- **Invariants:** one per environment (AD-14); `waiting` names each comparison or tournament chart not made and the seeds, settings or grids blocking it (EVAL-04, TOUR-03); `done` exactly when EVAL-07 holds for `env`, else `not_done` names each gap\n- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Report/ · **Cases:** Snake done with an explained tie; waiting on two seeds; the tank not done because short-view PPO loses to the flee bot; predator-prey not done because rung 3's no-trend is unexplained\n- **Used by:** cli\n\n### compare.Chart: type\n`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Chart: env: str; kind: Literal[\"curve\", \"tournament\"]; learner: str; setup: str; side: str | None; path: Path`\n- **Invariants:** a `curve` per learner, setup and learning side with complete runs, the baseline lines from the same setup and side (BASE-07, EVAL-03); a `tournament` per rung with 5 grids, `side` `None` (TOUR-03); `path` is a PNG under `reports/<env>/`, linked from that report (AD-09)\n- **Boundary:** compare · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.Chart/ · **Cases:** a tabular plain chart; a double-DQN shaped chart; a 5-fish wide-view PPO chart; a rung-1 prey chart; a rung-3 tournament chart\n- **Used by:** compare; PL-02\n\n### compare.build_report: Make every comparison\n`def build_report(*, runs: Path = Path(\"runs\"), tournaments: Path = Path(\"tournaments\"), out: Path = Path(\"reports\")) -> tuple[Report, ...]`\n- **Invariants:** one report per environment with runs (AD-14), every test through the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill on complete runs only, corrected within that report (AD-08); EVAL-04 for each learner setup's learning side against that side's baselines of the same setup (BASE-07); a pair whose settings differ beyond the added piece is refused (DQN-05, SHAPE-05, PG-05, COEV-06); top learner `pg-ppo` `fish-5-short`\n- **Predator-prey (28 tests):** EVAL-04 for each side of `control-<side>`, `rung-1`, `rung-2`, `rung-3` against that side's `random` and `scripted`; EVAL-05 per side `control-<side>`→`rung-1`→`rung-2`→`rung-3`, the control step with EVAL-08's note; two trend tests per rung (TOUR-04); top learners both sides of `rung-3`, plus every tournament (EVAL-07)\n- **Errors:** a refused or short pair, or a rung missing grids, goes to `waiting`, never raised\n- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/compare.build_report/ · **Cases:** 5 seeds of two Snake rungs and both baselines; neither means saved game 0; `side` picks which tested side's games `index` counts and which side plays a fresh seed, by default the run's first learning or recorded side\n- **Errors:** `ValueError` if both `index` and `seed` are given, or for a side the run didn't test; full text
I've drafted the architecture for predator-prey (S3). All 33 new requirements now have a home, and I reviewed the 16 changed ones. I'm stopping here for your approval.

**Commit:** there was nothing to commit. The spec, idea file, requests and roadmap changes were already in commit `ade4b5f` and the tree was clean.

**Gate checks:** `dt status` shows nothing uncovered, nothing changed since and nothing stale. `dt lint` shows 0 errors and the same 12 warnings as last session.

## What changed
- **Files:** `docs/architecture.md` and `docs/packages/rllab/contract.md`, both set back to **Draft**.
- **Shape:** still one package. The size trigger fires again: there are now 17 components, a fourth command (`rllab tournament`), and a 524-line contract. Predator-prey adds no new dependency and nothing that runs on its own, so I followed UR-34, UR-38 and UR-65 rather than ask you a fourth time.
- **New components:**
  - `hunt`: the predator-prey world.
  - `colearn`: the ladder rungs and controls, plus each side's pool of past copies.
  - `tournament`: plays every predator version of a run against every prey version.
- **Components that grew:** `baselines` gains the chaser and the forager, and the random swimmer now takes a top speed. `pg` covers COEV-01. `registry`, `train`, `store`, `compare`, `replay` and `cli` each take predator-prey.
- **New decisions (Proposed):**
  - **AD-16 — the core works in "sides":** every environment is one or more sides, each with its own view size, moves and score. Snake and the tank each have one side. This changes the M1 contracts again, the same way AD-11 did, before any code exists.
  - **AD-17 — co-learning is a schedule:** the training loop follows it for every environment and never knows the rungs. Snake and the tank use a schedule where their one side always learns.
  - **AD-18 — predator-prey is its own NumPy simulation:** it shares no code with the tank, so saved tank games can't shift (RUN-09).
  - **AD-19 — the tournament is a separate command:** its results are saved under `tournaments/`, so they stay out of each run's hour and its run folder doesn't change after its status is written.
- **Accepted decisions updated in place:** AD-01, 02, 04–12 and 14, to cover three environments and per-side seeds, files, charts and settings. None are superseded.
- **Contract:** `registry.make_agent` is retired and replaced by `make_agents`, which returns one agent per side. There are 21 new symbols.
- **Your words:** the design follows UR-80, UR-81, UR-86, UR-88, UR-91 and UR-92. No request was changed.

## ASSUMPTIONS
1. **Side order:** `predator` first, then `prey`. Snake's single side is named `snake` and the tank's is `fish`.
2. **Setup labels:** predator-prey learner runs are `pg-ppo` with `--match rung-1|rung-2|rung-3|control-predator|control-prey`. Baseline runs use `--side predator|prey` and play against the other side's scripted bot.
3. **Turn swaps (rung 2):** a side stops learning right after its 20th update, and the other side starts at the next game. Swaps never happen mid-game. Rung 3 is rung 2 plus the pool, so only one side learns in any game.
4. **Opponents in training:** frozen sides and pool copies pick their moves by sampling, the same way they trained. They don't play greedily.
5. **Versions:** the 10 versions are saved at every second curve point, the last at the end. Only ladder runs save them; controls don't.
6. **Tournament tanks:** the tournament uses test seeds 0–19. Each rung's two trend tests count as one result, which shows a trend only when both are significant.
7. **Test count:** the predator-prey report makes 28 tests: 16 baseline, 6 ladder and 6 trend.
8. **Pellets:** new pellets appear within 9.5 of the centre. Relative velocities are measured over the step just run, as in the tank.
9. **New learner interface:** `Learner` gains `updates` and `frozen()`, and every learner implements them.

## Open questions and risks
- **One hour per run:** a ladder run trains two policies and plays 200 test games at each of 20 curve points. The first predator-prey task times this before the step budget is set.
- **Tournament time:** about 30,000 games across the 15 ladder runs, untimed so far.
- **Knock-on work:** AD-16 and AD-17 mean the pipelines, test cases and M1's interface task all need redoing. That's `pipeline-design` (including a new tournament pipeline), `test-planning`, then `build-planning`, all before `build-setup`.

Do you approve, or would you like changes?

**Commit:** there was nothing to commit. The spec, idea file, requests and roadmap changes were already in commit `ade4b5f` and the tree was clean.

**Gate checks:** `dt status` shows nothing uncovered, nothing changed since and nothing stale. `dt lint` shows 0 errors and the same 12 warnings as last session.

## What changed
- **Files:** `docs/architecture.md` and `docs/packages/rllab/contract.md`, both set back to **Draft**.
- **Shape:** still one package. The size trigger fires again: there are now 17 components, a fourth command (`rllab tournament`), and a 524-line contract. Predator-prey adds no new dependency and nothing that runs on its own, so I followed UR-34, UR-38 and UR-65 rather than ask you a fourth time.
- **New components:**
  - `hunt`: the predator-prey world.
  - `colearn`: the ladder rungs and controls, plus each side's pool of past copies.
  - `tournament`: plays every predator version of a run against every prey version.
- **Components that grew:** `baselines` gains the chaser and the forager, and the random swimmer now takes a top speed. `pg` covers COEV-01. `registry`, `train`, `store`, `compare`, `replay` and `cli` each take predator-prey.
- **New decisions (Proposed):**
  - **AD-16 — the core works in "sides":** every environment is one or more sides, each with its own view size, moves and score. Snake and the tank each have one side. This changes the M1 contracts again, the same way AD-11 did, before any code exists.
  - **AD-17 — co-learning is a schedule:** the training loop follows it for every environment and never knows the rungs. Snake and the tank use a schedule where their one side always learns.
  - **AD-18 — predator-prey is its own NumPy simulation:** it shares no code with the tank, so saved tank games can't shift (RUN-09).
  - **AD-19 — the tournament is a separate command:** its results are saved under `tournaments/`, so they stay out of each run's hour and its run folder doesn't change after its status is written.
- **Accepted decisions updated in place:** AD-01, 02, 04–12 and 14, to cover three environments and per-side seeds, files, charts and settings. None are superseded.
- **Contract:** `registry.make_agent` is retired and replaced by `make_agents`, which returns one agent per side. There are 21 new symbols.
- **Your words:** the design follows UR-80, UR-81, UR-86, UR-88, UR-91 and UR-92. No request was changed.
